		public string[] BasisList => new string[2] { "Mass Fraction", "Mole Fraction" };

		public string SelectedBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_selectedBasis = value;
				if (_selectedBasis.Equals(BasisList[0]))
				{
					ABV_Basis = BasisType.BT_Mass;
				}
				else
				{
					ABV_Basis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				}
				OnPropertyChanged("SelectedBasis");
			}
		}

		public double? CompVal0
		{
			get
			{
				if (_compVal0 == -999999.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return _compVal0;
			}
			set
			{
				_compVal0 = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("CompVal0");
				ValidateCompVals();
			}
		}

		public double? CompVal1

		public AddByValue(BasisType _basis, string _compName0, string _compName1, string _compName2, Window owner = null)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			ABV_Basis = _basis;
			lblCompName0.Content = _compName0;
			lblCompName1.Content = _compName1;
			lblCompName2.Content = _compName2;
			if (owner != null)
			{
				base.Owner = owner;
			}
			else
			{
				new WindowInteropHelper(this).Owner = GetActiveWindow();
			}
			base.Loaded += AddByValue_Loaded;
		}

		private void AddByValue_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DataContext = this;
			if (ABV_Basis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
			{
				SelectedBasis = BasisList[0];
			}
			else
			{
				SelectedBasis = BasisList[1];
			}
		}

		private void ValidateCompVals()
		{
			double? num = 0.0;
			if (CompVal0.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal0;
			}
			if (CompVal1.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal1;
			}
			if (CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				num += CompVal2;
			}
			if (num > 1.0 || num < 1.0)
			{
				IsOKEnabled = false;
			}
			else
			{
				IsOKEnabled = true;
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal0_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal1.HasValue && CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				double? num = 1.0 - (CompVal1 + CompVal2);
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					CompVal0 = num;
					IsOKEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.SumGreater1, "Aspen Plus", (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal1_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal0.HasValue && CompVal2.HasValue)
			{
				double? num = 1.0 - (CompVal0 + CompVal2);
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					CompVal1 = num;
					IsOKEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.SumGreater1, "Aspen Plus", (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)64, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				}
			}
		}

		private void txtCompVal2_GotFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (CompVal0.HasValue && CompVal1.HasValue)

		public string SelectedPressureUnit
		{
			get
			{
				return _objConsep.m_sPressureUnits;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_objConsep.m_sPressureUnits != value)
				{
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "uom"));
					object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value");
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value", value, (object)_objConsep.m_dbPressure))
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value", text, obj);
						_objConsep.m_sPressureUnits = value;
						((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedPressureUnit");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public MMConSep_TernaryPlot PlotView => ((MMViewModel)this).View as MMConSep_TernaryPlot;

		public Collection<string> BasisItems => new Collection<string>
		{
			PlotFormsResources.Mole,
			PlotFormsResources.Mass
		};

		public string SelectedBasis
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedBasis;
			}
			set
			{
				_selectedBasis = value;
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedBasis");
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Basis_Prompt");
				if (_selectedBasis == PlotFormsResources.Mole)
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MoleBasis);
				}
				else
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MassBasis);
				}
				ChangeBasis();
			}
		}

		public string Basis_Prompt
		{
			get
			{
				if (SelectedBasis == PlotFormsResources.Mole)
				{
					return PlotFormsResources.MoleBasis;
				}
				return PlotFormsResources.MassBasis;
			}
		}

		[DllImport("MMUtil.Dll", CallingConvention = CallingConvention.StdCall)]
		public static extern void DCGetASCDDistil([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Interface)] out IDistil DistilAnalysis);

		private void SetReference()
		{
			_objLogic = new Logic();
			_objConsep = new Consep();
			_objConsep.PropPackage = _PropPackage;
			_objLogic.m_CConsep = _objConsep;
			_objResultData = new ResultData();
		}

		private bool InitConSep(IDistil engine = null)
		{
			//IL_0065: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02ef: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			SetReference();
			if (engine == null)
			{
				DCGetASCDDistil(out engine);
			}
			try
			{
				_DistillationApp = engine;
				_DistillationApp.InitFromAppdfFile("");
				_PropPackage = _DistillationApp.PropertyPackage;
				_objConsep.PropPackage = _DistillationApp.PropertyPackage;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return false;
			}
			_LocalIDiciallationMap = _DistillationApp.DistillationMap;
			_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap = _LocalIDiciallationMap;
			try
			{
				object vCompIds = new string[10];
				object vName = new string[10];
				object vMolwt = new double[10];
				_PropPackage.GetComponentList(ref vCompIds, ref vName, ref vMolwt);
				string[] array = (string[])vCompIds;
				_ = (string[])vName;
				double[] array2 = (double[])vMolwt;
				_PropPackage.GetPropMethodList(ref vCompIds);
				string[] array3 = (string[])vCompIds;
				_objConsep.m_MaxIter = _PropPackage.FlashMaxIter;
				_objConsep.m_FlashTol = _PropPackage.FlashTol;
				_objConsep.m_CurveCalcOption = (asCurveAlgorithm)_LocalIDiciallationMap.CurveCalcOption;
				_objConsep.m_CurveStepSize = _LocalIDiciallationMap.StepSizeCurve;
				_objConsep.m_EnvelopeStepSize = _LocalIDiciallationMap.StepSizeEnv;
				string[] array4 = new string[array3.Length - 3];
				for (int i = 3; i < array3.Length; i++)
				{
					array4[i - 3] = array3[i].Trim().Substring(0, 8);
				}
				string[] array5 = new string[array.Length];
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					array5[j] = array[j].Trim().Substring(0, 8);
				}
				for (int k = 0; k < array5.Length; k++)
				{
					for (int l = 1; l < 8; l++)
					{
						if (!array5[k].EndsWith(" "))
						{
							break;
						}
						array5[k] = array5[k].Substring(0, 8 - l);
					}
				}
				local_szPropModelList = new string[array4.Length];
				array4.CopyTo(local_szPropModelList, 0);
				local_szComp = new string[array5.Length];
				array5.CopyTo(local_szComp, 0);
				local_vMole = new double[array2.Length];
				array2.CopyTo(local_vMole, 0);
				_objConsep.m_szPropModelList = new string[array4.Length];
				array4.CopyTo(_objConsep.m_szPropModelList, 0);
				_objConsep.m_szComp = new string[array5.Length];
				array5.CopyTo(_objConsep.m_szComp, 0);
				_objConsep.m_vMole = new double[array2.Length];
				array2.CopyTo(_objConsep.m_vMole, 0);
				UpdateResultData();
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex2.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				return false;
			}
		}

		public void Get_Values_for_ConsepGUI(ref string[] maincomps, ref double PressureValue, ref string PressureUnit, Configuration Config, BasisType Basis, ref string tempunits, ref int mode, ref string Model, ref int Phases, ref double[] x_Feed, ref double[,] X_Out, ref string[] ID_X_Out, ref string BlockName, ref int nStreams, ref int[,] mEntered, ref double[] Distcomp, ref double[] distreco, ref double[] botcomp, ref double[] botreco, ref double[,] CompMap, ref double RefluxRatio, ref double ReboilerRatio, asRefReb ReflexReboiler, ref int[] vDecSpec, ref double DecVal, asEnthalpyMethod EnthalpyMethod, ref int Unmap, ref int MaxIter, ref double FlashTol, asCurveAlgorithm CurveAlgorithm, ref double CurveStep, ref double EnvStep, ref int MaxStages, asStopCriteria StopCriteria)
		{
			AzeoGUIGlobals.gbl_Initialised = true;
			totalcomp = _objConsep.m_szComp.Length;
			if (x_Feed == null || ID_X_Out == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			double[,] array = new double[totalcomp, 3];
			for (int i = 0; i < totalcomp; i++)
			{
				array[i, 0] = CompMap[i, 0];
				array[i, 1] = CompMap[i, 1];
				array[i, 2] = CompMap[i, 2];
				if (array[i, 0] < 0.0 || array[i, 0] > 1.0 || array[i, 0] == 0.0)
				{
					array[i, 0] = 0.0;
				}
				else
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (array[i, 1] < 0.0 || array[i, 1] > 1.0 || array[i, 1] == 0.0)
				{
					array[i, 1] = 0.0;
				}
				else
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (array[i, 2] < 0.0 || array[i, 2] > 1.0 || array[i, 2] == 0.0)
				{
					array[i, 2] = 0.0;
				}
				else
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (num < 3)
			{
				_objConsep.m_CompDistArray = null;
			}
			else
			{
				_objConsep.m_CompDistArray = array;
			}
			_objConsep.m_DistilComp = Distcomp;
			_objConsep.m_DistRecovery = distreco;
			_objConsep.m_BotComp = botcomp;
			_objConsep.m_BotRecovery = botreco;
			MTriGlobals.CopyCompositionForDisplay(_objConsep.m_DistilComp, _objConsep.m_BotComp);
			_objConsep.m_vPureCompNames = new string[maincomps.Length];
			for (int j = 0; j < maincomps.Length; j++)
			{
				_objConsep.m_vPureCompNames[j] = maincomps[j];
			}
			_objConsep.m_dbPressure = PressureValue;
			_objConsep.m_sPressureUnits = PressureUnit;
			_objConsep.m_Decanter = Config;
			localdecanter = Config;
			_objConsep.m_nBasis = Basis;
			if (Basis == BasisType.BT_Mass || Basis == BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				toolbarbasis = (int)Basis;
				SelectedBasis = ((Basis == BasisType.BT_Mass) ? PlotFormsResources.Mass : PlotFormsResources.Mole);
			}
			else
			{
				Basis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				toolbarbasis = 2;
				SelectedBasis = PlotFormsResources.Mole;
			}
			_objConsep.m_sDisplayTempUnits = tempunits;
			_objConsep.m_nMode = mode;
			_objConsep.m_iInteractive = 0;
			_objConsep.m_szPropModel = Model;
			_objConsep.m_szLLEModel = Model;
			_objConsep.m_szVLEModel = Model;
			_objConsep.m_nPhase = Phases;
			_objConsep.m_vInStreamData = x_Feed;
			_objConsep.m_sBlockName = BlockName;
			_objConsep.m_vOutStreamData = X_Out;
			_objConsep.m_RefluxReboiler = ReflexReboiler;
			_objConsep.m_InpReboilerRatio = ReboilerRatio;
			_objConsep.m_InpRefluxRatio = RefluxRatio;
			if (ReflexReboiler == asRefReb.asReboiler)
			{
				if (ReboilerRatio > 0.0)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.m_InpReboilerRatio = ReboilerRatio;
				}
				else
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.m_InpReboilerRatio = null;
				}
			}
			else if (RefluxRatio > 0.0)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.m_InpRefluxRatio = RefluxRatio;
			}
			else
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.m_InpRefluxRatio = null;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SPECTYPE", "value"));
			int num2 = 0;
			switch (text)
			{
			case "VAPOR-COMP":
				num2 = 1;
				break;
			case "REFLUX-COMP":
				num2 = 2;
				break;
			case "SP-REFLUX":
				num2 = 3;
				break;
			}
			int num3 = 0;
			text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.COMP", "value"));
			for (int k = 0; k < maincomps.Length; k++)
			{
				if (text == maincomps[k])
				{
					num3 = k + 1;
				}
			}
			if (vDecSpec == null || vDecSpec.Length == 0)
			{
				int[] vDecSpec2 = new int[2] { num2, num3 };
				_objConsep.m_vDecSpec = vDecSpec2;
				localvDecSpec = vDecSpec2;
			}
			else
			{
				_objConsep.m_vDecSpec = vDecSpec;
				localvDecSpec = vDecSpec;
				if (vDecSpec[1] > 0)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.DecanterComponent = "dummy";
				}
			}
			_objConsep.m_dbDecanterVal = DecVal;
			if (DecVal > 0.0 && DecVal != 1E+35)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.DecanterComposition = DecVal;
			}
			_objConsep.m_nEnthalpyMethod = EnthalpyMethod;
			localEnthalpyMethod = EnthalpyMethod;
			_objConsep.m_nUnMap = Unmap;
			localUnMap = Unmap;
			localMaxIter = MaxIter;
			if (_objConsep.PropPackage != null)
			{
				_objConsep.PropPackage.FlashMaxIter = MaxIter;
			}
			localFlashTol = FlashTol;
			if (_objConsep.PropPackage != null)
			{
				_objConsep.PropPackage.FlashTol = FlashTol;
			}
			localCurveAlgorithm = CurveAlgorithm;
			_objConsep.m_CurveCalcOption = CurveAlgorithm;
			localCurveStep = CurveStep;
			_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeCurve = CurveStep;
			localEnvStep = EnvStep;
			_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.StepSizeEnv = EnvStep;
			localMaxStages = MaxStages;
			_objConsep.m_nMaxStages = MaxStages;
			localStopCriteria = StopCriteria;
			_objConsep.m_nStopCriteria = StopCriteria;
			localmEntered = mEntered.Clone() as int[,];
			localDistComp = Distcomp.Clone() as double[];
			localDistReco = distreco.Clone() as double[];
			localBotComp = botcomp.Clone() as double[];
			localBotReco = botreco.Clone() as double[];
			_objConsep.m_szDistillateStream = ID_X_Out[0];
			_objConsep.m_szBottomsStream = ID_X_Out[1];
			Maincomponents = maincomps.Clone() as string[];
			xfeed = x_Feed.Clone() as double[];
			xout = X_Out.Clone() as double[,];
			idxout = ID_X_Out.Clone() as string[];
			pressureval = PressureValue;
			localpressureunit = PressureUnit;
			confg = Config;
			tempunit = tempunits;
			localmode = mode;
			localmodel = Model;
			localphase = Phases;
			localblocknm = BlockName;
			localstreamnos = nStreams;
			localbasis = Basis;
			localCompDistArray = CompMap.Clone() as double[,];
			MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.xy;
			localRefluxRatio = RefluxRatio;
			localReboilerRatio = ReboilerRatio;
			localReflexReboiler = ReflexReboiler;
			localDecVal = DecVal;
			int num4 = 0;
			int[,] array2 = new int[3, 4];
			int[,] array3 = new int[3, 4];
			for (int l = 0; l < 3; l++)
			{
				for (int m = 0; m < 4; m++)
				{
					if (mEntered[l, m] != 0)
					{
						num4++;
						array2[l, m] = mEntered[l, m];
						if (mEntered[l, m] == 0)
						{
							array3[l, m] = 1;
						}
						else
						{
							array3[l, m] = 0;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_objConsep.m_nMatTot = num4;
			_objConsep.m_Entered = array2.Clone() as int[,];
			_objConsep.m_Enabled = array3.Clone() as int[,];
			if (AzeoGUIGlobals.gbl_Initialised)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ReqInputs.FireEvents = true;
			}
		}

			{
				stopCriteria = asStopCriteria.asiStopAdvance;
			}
			Get_Values_for_ConsepGUI(ref maincomps, ref PressureValue, ref PressureUnit, config, basisType, ref tempunits, ref mode, ref Model, ref Phases, ref x_Feed, ref X_Out, ref ID_X_Out, ref BlockName, ref nStreams, ref mEntered, ref Distcomp, ref distreco, ref botcomp, ref botreco, ref CompMap, ref RefluxRatio, ref ReboilerRatio, reflexReboiler, ref vDecSpec, ref DecVal, enthalpyMethod, ref Unmap, ref MaxIter, ref FlashTol, curveAlgorithm, ref CurveStep, ref EnvStep, ref MaxStages, stopCriteria);
			UpdateDisplayedData();
		}

		private void UpdateDisplayedData()
		{
			if (_objConsep.m_RefluxReboiler == asRefReb.asReflux)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("txtDesign_var1.Value", (object)_objConsep.m_InpRefluxRatio);
			}
			else
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("txtDesign_var1.Value", (object)_objConsep.m_InpReboilerRatio);
			}
			if (_objConsep.m_Decanter == Configuration.Decanter)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("MMLBUText1_var1.Value", (object)_objConsep.m_dbDecanterVal);
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("lbtuPres_var1.Value", (object)_objConsep.m_dbPressure);
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "uom_options"));
			if (array != null)
			{
				PressUnitsList.Clear();
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string item in array2)
				{
					PressUnitsList.Add(item);
				}
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedPressureUnit");
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("txtMvVal_var0.Value", ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.@p_txtMvVal0", "value"));
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("txtMvVal_var1.Value", ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.@p_txtMvVal1", "value"));
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("txtMvVal_var2.Value", ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.@p_txtMvVal2", "value"));
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("optPhase"));
			if (text == "VLL")
			{
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10] = true;
				_objLogic.Recalc[10] = true;
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[9] = false;
				_objLogic.Recalc[9] = false;
			}
			else if (text == "LL")
			{
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10] = false;
				_objLogic.Recalc[10] = false;
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[9] = true;
				_objLogic.Recalc[9] = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10] = false;
				_objLogic.Recalc[10] = false;
				_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[9] = false;
				_objLogic.Recalc[9] = false;
			}
			if (CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("optCurve")) == "RES")
			{
				_objConsep.CurveType = Curve.Residue;
			}
			else
			{
				_objConsep.CurveType = Curve.Distillation;
			}
		}

		private void Calculate()
		{
			_objConsep.ErrorStore = ErrorStore;
			_objLogic.CalcObjects();
			_objConsep.ErrorStore = null;
			UpdateResultData();
		}

		private void MatBalValues()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("enabled_status") is int[,] enabled)
			{
				m_enabled = enabled;
			}
			m_vPureCompNames[0] = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SELCOMP1", "value"));
			m_vPureCompNames[1] = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SELCOMP2", "value"));
			m_vPureCompNames[2] = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SELCOMP3", "value"));
			CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Output.STREAMID.2", "value"));
			CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Output.STREAMID.3", "value"));
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "cboBasis_MultiMap.Value"));
			text = ((!(text == "cboBasis_MultiMapOption_1")) ? "MA" : "MO");
			string[] array = new string[3];
			string[] array2 = new string[3];
			string[] array3 = new string[3];
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
				{
					if (m_enabled[i, j] != 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					switch (i)
					{
					case 0:
						switch (j)
						{
						case 0:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[0];
							array3[num] = $"DISTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 1:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[0];
							array3[num] = $"BOTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 2:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[0];
							array3[num] = "DISTRECO1";
							break;
						case 3:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[0];
							array3[num] = "BOTRECO1";
							break;
						}
						break;
					case 1:
						switch (j)
						{
						case 0:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[1];
							array3[num] = $"DISTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 1:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[1];
							array3[num] = $"BOTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 2:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[1];
							array3[num] = "DISTRECO2";
							break;
						case 3:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[1];
							array3[num] = "BOTRECO2";
							break;
						}
						break;
					case 2:
						switch (j)
						{
						case 0:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[2];
							array3[num] = $"DISTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 1:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Composition);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[2];
							array3[num] = $"BOTC{i + 1}{text}";
							break;
						case 2:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.DistillateStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[2];
							array3[num] = "DISTRECO3";
							break;
						case 3:
							array[num] = PlotFormsResources.BottomStream;
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"p_lblCompRec{num}", (object)PlotFormsResources.Recovery);
							array2[num] = m_vPureCompNames[2];
							array3[num] = "BOTRECO3";
							break;
						}
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblDes0", (object)array[0]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblDes1", (object)array[1]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblDes2", (object)array[2]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblComp0", (object)array2[0]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblComp1", (object)array2[1]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_lblComp2", (object)array2[2]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_txtMvVal0", (object)array3[0]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_txtMvVal1", (object)array3[1]);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_txtMvVal2", (object)array3[2]);
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			if (pd.Name == "txtDesign_var1" && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "txtDesign_var1.Value"));
				if (num.HasValue)
				{
					if (_objConsep.m_RefluxReboiler == asRefReb.asReflux)
					{
						_objConsep.m_InpRefluxRatio = num.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						_objConsep.m_InpReboilerRatio = num.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (pd.Name == "MMLBUText1_var1" && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "MMLBUText1_var1.Value"));
				if (num2.HasValue)
				{
					_objConsep.m_dbDecanterVal = num2.Value;
				}
			}
			else if (pd.Name == "lbtuPres_var1" && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				double? num3 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "lbtuPres_var1.Value"));
				object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value");
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "uom"));
				if (!obj.Equals(num3))
				{
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value", _objConsep.m_sPressureUnits, (object)num3))
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value", text, obj);
						_objConsep.m_dbPressure = num3.Value;
					}
					else
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateViewPropertyPath("lbtuPres_var1.Value", (object)_objConsep.m_dbPressure);
					}
				}
			}
			else if ((pd.Name == "txtMvVal_var0" || pd.Name == "txtMvVal_var1" || pd.Name == "txtMvVal_var2") && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				double? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, pd.Name + ".Value"));
				string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter($"p_txtMvVal{pd.Name[pd.Name.Length - 1]}"));
				if (text2.StartsWith("DISTC"))
				{
					if (_objConsep.m_DistilComp != null)
					{
						int num5 = int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 3].ToString()) - 1;
						if (num4.HasValue)
						{
							_objConsep.m_DistilComp[num5] = num4.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							_objConsep.m_DistilComp[num5] = -999999.0;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("DISTRECO"))
				{
					int num6 = int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 1].ToString()) - 1;
					if (_objConsep.m_DistRecovery != null)
					{
						if (num4.HasValue)
						{
							_objConsep.m_DistRecovery[num6] = num4.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							_objConsep.m_DistRecovery[num6] = -999999.0;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("BOTC"))
				{
					int num7 = int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 3].ToString()) - 1;
					if (_objConsep.m_BotComp != null)
					{
						if (num4.HasValue)
						{
							_objConsep.m_BotComp[num7] = num4.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							_objConsep.m_BotComp[num7] = -999999.0;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("BOTRECO"))
				{
					int num8 = int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 1].ToString()) - 1;
					if (_objConsep.m_BotComp != null)
					{
						if (num4.HasValue)
						{
							_objConsep.m_BotRecovery[num8] = num4.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							_objConsep.m_BotRecovery[num8] = -999999.0;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (_cmdRecalculate_Command != null)
			{
				_cmdRecalculate_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.IBASIS", "value"));
				if (text == "MOLE")
				{
					text = PlotFormsResources.Mole;
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MoleBasis);
				}
				else
				{
					text = PlotFormsResources.Mass;
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MassBasis);
				}
				MateriaBalanceHeader = string.Format(PlotFormsResources.MateriaBalanceHeader, text);
				SelectedBasis = text;
				string text2 = "Relux ratio";
				object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveResource("MMConSep_Input_Spec_ViewModel__CxvMultiMapOption_Display_Reflux_ratio");
				if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()))
				{
					text2 = obj.ToString();
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("RefluxRatio", (object)text2);
				string text3 = "Reboil ratio";
				obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveResource("MMConSep_Input_Spec_ViewModel__CxvMultiMapOption_Display_Reboil_ratio");
				if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()))
				{
					text3 = obj.ToString();
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("ReboilRatio", (object)text3);
				string text4 = "Vapor composition121";
				obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveResource("MMConSep_Input_Spec_ViewModel__CxvMultiMapOption_Display_Column_vapor_composition");
				if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()))
				{
					text4 = obj.ToString();
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("VaporComposition", (object)text4);
				string text5 = "Reflux composition";
				obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveResource("MMConSep_Input_Spec_ViewModel__CxvMultiMapOption_Display_Reflux_composition");
				if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()))
				{
					text5 = obj.ToString();
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("RefluxComposition", (object)text5);
				string text6 = "Single phase reflux";
				obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveResource("MMConSep_Input_Spec_ViewModel__CxvMultiMapOption_Display_Single_phase_reflux");
				if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()))
				{
					text6 = obj.ToString();
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("SinglePhase", (object)text6);
				MatBalValues();
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		public void SetObject()
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0035: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0070: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0087: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0091: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bf: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMConSep_TernaryPlot mMConSep_TernaryPlot)
			{
				_plot = mMConSep_TernaryPlot.pfsplot;
				_plot.OnClick += new _DPfsplotEvents_OnClickEventHandler(_plot_OnClick);
				_plot.OnRBClick += new _DPfsplotEvents_OnRBClickEventHandler(_plot_OnRBClick);
				_plot.RegionDefined += _plot_RegionDefined;
				_plot.PointerLocation += new _DPfsplotEvents_PointerLocationEventHandler(_plot_PointerLocation);
				_plot.KeyUpEvent += new _DPfsplotEvents_KeyUpEventHandler(_plot_KeyUpEvent);
				_plot.PointerExit += _plot_PointerExit;
				_plot.Scrolled += new _DPfsplotEvents_ScrolledEventHandler(_plot_Scrolled);
			}
			_plotArea = new PlotArea();
			_plotArea.PlotControlPtr = _plot;
			_plotArea.InitPlot();
			gridButton = false;
			_plotArea.Grid = gridButton;
		}

		protected override void OnViewChanged()
		{
			if (!InitConSep())
			{
				cmdCancel_Command_Execute(null);
				return;
			}
			SetObject();
			GetInputData();
			Calculate();
			CreateGraph();
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewChanged();
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
		}

		public override void OnViewClosed()
		{
			if (!_isCancelled)
			{
				Save();
			}
			_objLogic = null;
			_objConsep.PropPackage = null;
			_objConsep = null;
			_objResultData = null;
			_LocalIDiciallationMap = null;
			_PropPackage = null;
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewClosed();
		}

		private void CreateGraph(bool isRecalculating = false)
		{
			if (!isRecalculating)
			{
				if (CxsConverter.ValueToString(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "cboBasis_MultiMap.Value")) == "cboBasis_MultiMapOption_1")
				{
					MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				}
				else
				{
					MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mass;
				}
				CorrectBasis();
			}
			_plotArea.PlotDisplayUnit = _objResultData.m_sDisplayTempUnits;
			_plotArea.BasisType = (BasisType)_objResultData.m_nBasis;
			switch (_objResultData.m_sDisplayTempUnits)
			{
			case "R":
				IsStdTempUnitsRCommandCanExecute = false;
				break;
			case "C":
				IsStdTempUnitsCCommandCanExecute = false;
				break;
			case "F":
				IsStdTempUnitsFCommandCanExecute = false;
				break;
			case "K":
				IsStdTempUnitsKCommandCanExecute = false;
				break;
			}
			SetLabels();
			_plotArea.SetCoordinates();
			_plotArea.Redraw();
			CodisplayLabels();
			if (_plotArea.PlotType == PlotType.RightAngled)
			{
				_plotArea.PlotType = PlotType.Equilateral;
				ChangePlotImage = GetImage("3d.ico");
			}
			else if (_plotArea.PlotType == PlotType.Equilateral)
			{
				_plotArea.PlotType = PlotType.RightAngled;
				ChangePlotImage = GetImage("triright.ico");
			}
			SwapPlotType();
			Is_btnZoomIn_Command_Enabled = false;
			Is_btnZoomOut_Command_Enabled = false;
			Is_btnZoomFull_Command_Enabled = false;
			Clear_plot();
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MatBalLine);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkMBProd);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TextMBProd);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkFeed);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TextFeed);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineDecanter);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.YTopMarker);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.YTopLabel);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.FeasibleStrip);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.FeasibleRetify);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.Strip);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.StripMarker);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.Rectify);
			_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.RectifyMarker);
			Plot_Mbalance();
			Plot_Distillate();
			Plot_Bottoms();
			Plot_Feed();
			Plot_Decanter();
			Plot_YTop();
			Plot_FeasibleRectifyCurve();
			Plot_FeasibleStripCurve();
			Plot_RectifyCurve();
			Plot_StripCurve();
			if (_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[0] && _objLogic.Recalc[0])
			{
				Plot_Azeotrope();
				_objLogic.Recalc[0] = false;
			}
			if (_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[1] && _objLogic.Recalc[1])
			{
				Plot_DistillationBoundry();
				_objLogic.Recalc[1] = false;
			}
			if (_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10] && _objLogic.Recalc[10])
			{
				Plot_VLLEenvelope();
				_objLogic.Recalc[10] = false;
			}
			if (_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[9] && _objLogic.Recalc[9])
			{
				Plot_LLEenvelope();
				_objLogic.Recalc[9] = false;
			}
			_plotArea.Refresh();
			StageDispLabels();
			if (_showHideDlg == null)
			{
				_showHideDlg = new ShowHideDlg(this);
				_showHideDlg.InitializeState();
			}
			DisplayItem((_showHideDlg.DataContext as ShowHideDlgViewModel).IsCVFeedLabelsChecked, ObjType.TextFeed);
		}

		public void ChangeBasis()
		{
			if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mass;
			}
			else
			{
				MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
			}
			CorrectBasis();
			if (!((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				_plotArea.BasisType = MTriGlobals.TPBasis;
				_plotArea.Redraw();
				_objResultData.m_nBasis = (long)MTriGlobals.TPBasis;
			}
		}

		private void btnTieLine_Command_Execute(object args)
		{
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			IsBtnCurveChecked = false;
			IsBtnMarkerChecked = false;
			IsBtnDrawLineChecked = false;
			if (IsBtnTieLineChecked)
			{
				TieLineAdd_Start(bAddMany: true);
			}
			else
			{
				FinishedAdding();
			}
		}

		private bool btnTieLine_Command_CanExecute(object args)
		{
			return Is_btnTieLine_Command_Enabled;
		}

		private void _btnTieLineValue_Command_Execute(object args)
		{
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			IsBtnCurveChecked = false;
			IsBtnTieLineChecked = false;
			IsBtnMarkerChecked = false;
			IsBtnDrawLineChecked = false;
			TieLineAdd_Start(bAddMany: true);
			TieLineAddValue_Start();
		}

		private void btnGridToggle_Command_Execute(object args)
		{
			if (gridButton)
			{
				_plotArea.Grid = false;
				gridButton = false;
			}
			else
			{
				_plotArea.Grid = true;
				gridButton = true;
			}
		}

		private void cmdReset_CommandExecute(object args)
		{
			string selectedBasis = SelectedBasis;
			GetInputData();
			UpdateDisplayedData();
			Calculate();
			CreateGraph();
			if (SelectedBasis != selectedBasis)
			{
				SelectedBasis = selectedBasis;
			}
		}

		private void cmdRecalculate_CommandExecute(object args)
		{
			Calculate();
			CreateGraph(isRecalculating: true);
			CorrectBasis();
			_plotArea.BasisType = MTriGlobals.TPBasis;
			_plotArea.Redraw();
			_objResultData.m_nBasis = (long)MTriGlobals.TPBasis;
			_showHideDlg = new ShowHideDlg(this);
			_showHideDlg.Top = -1000.0;
			_showHideDlg.Left = -1000.0;
			_showHideDlg.Show();
			_showHideDlg.Hide();
		}

		private bool cmdRecalculate_CommandCanExecute(object args)
		{
			double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "txtDesign_var1.Value"));
			double? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "MMLBUText1_var1.Value"));
			double? num3 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "lbtuPres_var1.Value"));
			double? num4 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "txtMvVal_var0.Value"));
			double? num5 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "txtMvVal_var1.Value"));
			double? num6 = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)this, "txtMvVal_var2.Value"));
			bool flag = num.HasValue && num3.HasValue && num4.HasValue && num5.HasValue && num6.HasValue;
			object obj = cboColumn_var1;
			CxvVariableData val = (CxvVariableData)((obj is CxvVariableData) ? obj : null);
			if (val != null && ((CxvStatePropertyData)val).IsEnabled.HasValue && ((CxvStatePropertyData)val).IsEnabled.Value)
			{
				flag = flag && num2.HasValue;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private void cmdShowHide_CommandExecute(object args)
		{
			_showHideDlg = new ShowHideDlg(this);
			_showHideDlg.Owner = Window.GetWindow((DependencyObject)(object)PlotView);
			_showHideDlg.ShowDialog();
		}

			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
		}

		private void Save()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "value", (object)_objConsep.m_dbPressure);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.P_SPEC", "uom", (object)_objConsep.m_sPressureUnits);
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.IBASIS", "value"));
			_ = text == "MASS";
			for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
			{
				string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter($"p_txtMvVal{i}"));
				object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath($"txtMvVal_var{i}.Value", (CxvPropertyWatch)null);
				if (text2.StartsWith("DISTC"))
				{
					int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 3].ToString());
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text2}", "value", obj);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("DISTRECO"))
				{
					int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 1].ToString());
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text2}", "value", obj);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("BOTC"))
				{
					int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 3].ToString());
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text2}", "value", obj);
				}
				else if (text2.StartsWith("BOTRECO"))
				{
					int.Parse(text2[text2.Length - 1].ToString());
					((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text2}", "value", obj);
				}
			}
			if (_objConsep.m_RefluxReboiler == asRefReb.asReflux)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text}_RR", "value", (object)_objConsep.m_InpRefluxRatio);
			}
			else
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet($"@main.Input.{text}_BR", "value", (object)_objConsep.m_InpReboilerRatio);
			}
			if (_objConsep.m_Decanter == Configuration.Decanter)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.VALUE", "value", (object)_objConsep.m_dbDecanterVal);
			}
		}

		private void cmdCancel_Command_Execute(object args)
		{
			_isCancelled = true;
			((MMViewModel)this).CloseForm();
		}

		private void _plot_OnClick(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_OnClickEvent e)
		{
			//IL_02c8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02cd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02d9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02e5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02ec: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02f9: Expected O, but got Unknown
			try
			{
				_plotArea.BasisType = (BasisType)_objResultData.m_nBasis;
				if (_plotArea.LastID == null)
				{
					_plotArea.LastID = new MarkerObj();
				}
				IPlotobj plotobj = null;
				if (_plotArea.Highlight && _plotArea.LastID.iObjID != -1)
				{
					plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(_plotArea.LastID.iObjID + _plotArea.iIDOffset);
					if (plotobj != null && plotobj.ObjVisible)
					{
						_plot.SetObject((short)(_plotArea.LastID.iObjID + _plotArea.iIDOffset), (short)3, (short)4);
						_plotArea.Highlight = false;
					}
				}
				switch (_plotArea.UserMode)
				{
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingMarkerPoint:
					MarkerAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingCurvePoint:
					CurveAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLine:
					DrawLine_Do(e.ix, e.iy);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd:
					DrawLine_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				case DoingWhat.DW_SelectingTieLinePoint:
					TieLineAdd_End(e.x, e.y);
					break;
				}
				if (e.objectID != 0)
				{
					plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(e.objectID);
					if (plotobj != null && plotobj.iObjID != -1 && plotobj.ObjVisible)
					{
						switch (plotobj.eObjType)
						{
						case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
						case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
							if (_plotArea.FindObjByID(plotobj.iObjID + 1).ObjVisible)
							{
								_plot.SetObject((short)(plotobj.iObjID + 1), (short)5, (short)5);
								_plotArea.LastID = plotobj;
								_plotArea.Highlight = true;
								_plotArea.iIDOffset = 1;
							}
							break;
						case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
						case ObjType.MarkVLLEUser:
							if (_plotArea.FindObjByID(plotobj.iObjID - 1).ObjVisible)
							{
								_plot.SetObject((short)(plotobj.iObjID - 1), (short)5, (short)5);
								_plotArea.LastID = plotobj;
								_plotArea.Highlight = true;
								_plotArea.iIDOffset = -1;
							}
							_plotArea.Refresh();
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (e.objectID > 0)
				{
					_plotArea.LastID = plotobj;
				}
				if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TextMBProd)
				{
					_plotArea.ShowBalloon("Material Balance Tieline");
				}
				if (!_plot.IsRegionDefined())
				{
					IsStdZoomInCommandCanExecute = false;
				}
				_ = e.objectID;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ShowMessageBox(new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Message = ex.Message,
					Title = ((MMViewModel)this).GetProductName(),
					MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OK,
					MsgBoxImage = MessageBoxImage.Exclamation
				});
			}
		}

		private void _plot_OnRBClick(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_OnRBClickEvent e)
		{
			PlotView.plotHost.Focusable = true;
			PlotView.plotHost.Focus();
			_plotArea.LastID = _plotArea.FindObjByID(e.objectID);
			if (_plotArea.LastID != null && _plotArea.LastID.iObjID > 0)
			{
				if (!_plotArea.LastID.ObjVisible)
				{
					return;
				}
				switch (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType)
				{
				case ObjType.MarkUserPoint:
				{
					System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = ((FrameworkElement)(object)PlotView).Resources["mnuMarker"] as System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu;
					if (_plotArea.LastID.vUserData != null && double.Parse(_plotArea.LastID.vUserData.ToString()) != 0.0)
					{
						for (int i = 1; i <= 2; i++)
						{
							(contextMenu.Items[i] as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
						}
						(contextMenu.Items[3] as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
					}
					else
					{
						for (int j = 1; j <= 2; j++)
						{
							(contextMenu.Items[j] as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem).IsEnabled = true;
						}
						(contextMenu.Items[3] as System.Windows.Controls.MenuItem).IsEnabled = false;
					}
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu = contextMenu;
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				}
				case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
				case ObjType.MarkVLLEUser:
				case ObjType.TieLineLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
				case ObjType.FreeHandLine:
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu = ((FrameworkElement)(object)PlotView).Resources["mnuTieline"] as System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu;
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveResidue:
				case ObjType.CurveDistill:
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu = ((FrameworkElement)(object)PlotView).Resources["mnuCurve"] as System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu;
					PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
					break;
				case ObjType.EnvLLE:
				case ObjType.EnvVLLE:
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu = ((FrameworkElement)(object)PlotView).Resources["mnuStandard"] as System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu;
				PlotView.plotHost.ContextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private void _plot_RegionDefined(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			IsStdZoomInCommandCanExecute = true;
			if (_plotArea.ZoomMode == 0.0)
			{
				IsStdZoomOutCommandCanExecute = false;
				IsStdZoomFullCommandCanExecute = false;
			}
			Is_btnZoomIn_Command_Enabled = true;
		}

		private void _plot_PointerLocation(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_PointerLocationEvent e)
		{
			if (_plotArea.UserMode == DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd)
			{
				_plot.DragLine(MTriGlobals.LineStart.X, MTriGlobals.LineStart.Y, e.ix, e.iy, (short)(-1));
			}
			string[] array = e.coordinate.Split('\n');
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				array[i] = array[i].Substring(2, array[i].Length - 2).Replace(".", MaskingAndValidatingHelper.GetSystemDecimalSymbol());
			}
			float num = float.Parse(array[0], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			float num2 = float.Parse(array[1], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			float num3 = float.Parse(array[2], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			if (_plotArea.CoDisplay == 1)
			{
				PlotView.lBasis.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				((UIElement)(object)PlotView.ComponentNames).Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				string text = num.ToString("#0.000") + Environment.NewLine + num2.ToString("#0.000") + Environment.NewLine + num3.ToString("#0.000");
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_codisp", (object)text);
			}
			else
			{
				PlotView.lBasis.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				((UIElement)(object)PlotView.ComponentNames).Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				((UIElement)(object)PlotView.codisp).Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			_plotArea.LastPX = num;
			_plotArea.LastPY = num2;
			_plotArea.LastPZ = 1f - num2 - num;
			_plotArea.LastMX = e.ix;
			_plotArea.LastMY = e.iy;
		}

		private void _plot_KeyUpEvent(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_KeyUpEvent e)
		{
			IPlotobj plotobj = null;
			if ((e.keyCode == 46 || e.keyCode == 8) && _plotArea.LastID.iObjID > 0)
			{
				if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TextTemperature || _plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TextFeed || _plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TextMBProd || _plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.YTopLabel)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TieLineVLLE || _plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE)
				{
					plotobj = _plotArea.LastID;
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(plotobj);
					plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(plotobj.iObjID + 1);
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(plotobj);
					_plotArea.Highlight = false;
					plotobj = null;
				}
				else if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.MarkVLLE || _plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.MarkVLLEUser)
				{
					plotobj = _plotArea.LastID;
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(plotobj);
					plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(plotobj.iObjID - 1);
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(plotobj);
					_plotArea.Highlight = false;
					plotobj = null;
				}
				else if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.MarkUserPoint)
				{
					RemoveLabelFromMarker(_plotArea.LastID);
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(_plotArea.LastID);
				}
				else if (_plotArea.LastID.eObjType == ObjType.TextUser)
				{
					int iObjID = _plotArea.LastID.iObjID;
					RemoveLabelFromMarker(_plotArea.FindLabelsParent(iObjID));
				}
				else
				{
					_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(_plotArea.LastID);
				}
				_plotArea.LastID = null;
				_plotArea.Refresh();
			}
			if (e.keyCode == 27)
			{
				FinishedAdding();
			}
		}

		private void _plot_PointerExit(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			string text = "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--" + Environment.NewLine + "-.--";
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_codisp", (object)text);
		}

		private void _plot_Scrolled(object sender, _DPfsplotEvents_ScrolledEvent e)
		{
			_plotArea.Refresh();
		}

		private BitmapImage GetImage(string imageName)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
			stringBuilder.Append(typeof(MMConSep_TernaryPlot_ViewModel).Assembly.FullName);
			stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
			stringBuilder.Append(imageName);
			return new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
		}

		private void CorrectBasis()
		{
			if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MassBasis);
				ChangeBasisHeader = PlotFormsResources.ChangeToMolarBasis;
			}
			else
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_basis", (object)PlotFormsResources.MoleBasis);
				ChangeBasisHeader = PlotFormsResources.ChangeToMassBasis;
			}
		}

		private void SetLabels()
		{
			switch (MTriGlobals.CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.xy:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Right, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[2]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Top, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[1]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureCompNames[0]);
				_plotArea.SetPlotLabelTemp(PAxis.A_Left, _objResultData.m_vPureTemps[0]);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void CodisplayLabels()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_compNames", (object)(_plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right) + Environment.NewLine + _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top) + Environment.NewLine + _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left)));
		}

		public bool Clear_plot()
		{
			if (_objLogic.DelCase[0] || _objLogic.DelCase[2] || _objLogic.DelCase[3] || _objLogic.DelCase[1])
			{
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.EnvLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.EnvVLLE);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveResidue);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveDistill);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveUser);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TextTemperature);
				_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TextUser);
				if (_objLogic.DelCase[0])
				{
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkUserPoint);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.FreeHandLine);
				}
				for (int i = 0; i <= 11; i++)
				{
					_objLogic.DelCase[i] = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (_objLogic.DelCase[5])
				{
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TextTemperature);
					_objLogic.DelCase[5] = false;
				}
				if (_objLogic.DelCase[6])
				{
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
					_objLogic.DelCase[6] = false;
				}
				if (_objLogic.DelCase[10] || _objLogic.DelCase[11] || _objLogic.DelCase[4])
				{
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.MarkVLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.EnvLLE);
					_plotArea.ClearObjs(ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					_objLogic.DelCase[4] = false;
					_objLogic.DelCase[10] = false;
					_objLogic.DelCase[11] = false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_Mbalance()
		{
			IPlotobj plotobj = null;
			plotobj = new CurveObj();
			_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.MatBalLine);
			plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 2;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, i, (_objResultData.m_vDistStream as double[])[i]);
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(1, i, (_objResultData.m_vbotStream as double[])[i]);
			}
			plotobj.AddObj();
			_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_Distillate()
		{
			try
			{
				if (_objResultData.m_DistilComp != null && _objResultData.m_DistilComp.Length >= 3)
				{
					IPlotobj plotobj = new MarkerObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.MarkMBProd);
					plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
					{
						if (_objResultData.m_DistilComp[i] < 0.0 || _objResultData.m_DistilComp[i] > 1.0)
						{
							return false;
						}
						plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, i, _objResultData.m_DistilComp[i]);
					}
					plotobj.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
					int iObjID = plotobj.iObjID;
					plotobj = new LabelObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.TextMBProd);
					plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 1;
					for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
					{
						plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, j, _objResultData.m_DistilComp[j]);
					}
					plotobj.vUserData = _objResultData.m_szDistillateStream;
					plotobj.AddObj();
					iObjID = plotobj.iObjID;
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
					plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(iObjID);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_Azeotrope()

				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_DistillationBoundry()

				for (int i = 0; i <= array.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
				{
					IPlotobj plotobj = new CurveObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
					plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = array.GetLength(1);
					for (int j = 0; j <= array.GetUpperBound(1); j++)
					{

						{
							plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(j, k, array[i, j, k]);
						}
					}
					plotobj.vUserData = array2[i];
					plotobj.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
				}
				_objLogic.DelCase[1] = false;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_VLLEenvelope()
		{
			try
			{
				double[,,] array = _objResultData.m_vXCurve as double[,,];
				for (int i = 0; i <= array.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
				{
					IPlotobj plotobj = new EnvelopeObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
					plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = array.GetLength(1);
					for (int j = 0; j <= array.GetUpperBound(1); j++)
					{
						for (int k = 0; k <= 2; k++)
						{
							plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(j, k, array[i, j, k]);
						}
					}
					plotobj.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
				}
				double[,] array2 = _objResultData.m_vTieLines as double[,];
				double[,] array3 = _objResultData.m_vVaporPoints as double[,];
				int num = 0;
				for (int l = 0; l <= array2.GetUpperBound(0); l += 2)
				{
					IPlotobj plotobj2 = new CurveObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj2, ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
					plotobj2.iNumberOfPoints = 2;
					for (int m = 0; m <= 1; m++)
					{
						for (int n = 0; n <= 2; n++)
						{
							plotobj2.SetdbPointsArr(m, n, array2[l + m, n]);
						}
					}
					plotobj2.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj2);
					plotobj2 = new MarkerObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj2, ObjType.MarkVLLE);
					plotobj2.iNumberOfPoints = 1;
					for (int num2 = 0; num2 <= 2; num2++)
					{
						plotobj2.SetdbPointsArr(0, num2, array3[num, num2]);
					}
					plotobj2.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj2);
					num++;
				}
				_objLogic.DelCase[10] = false;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Plot_LLEenvelope()

		}

		private void StageDispLabels()
		{
			double num = _objResultData.m_dbV_rect / _objResultData.m_dbFlow;
			double num2 = _objResultData.m_dbV_strip / _objResultData.m_dbFlow;
			double num3 = ((!(num > num2)) ? num2 : num);
			if (_objResultData.m_nfeasible == 1)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_stages", (object)(PlotFormsResources.Stage + " " + _objResultData.m_dbStages_Total.ToString("#0.0") + Environment.NewLine + PlotFormsResources.FeedStage + " " + _objResultData.m_dbStages_Feed.ToString("#0.0") + Environment.NewLine + PlotFormsResources.VF + " " + num3.ToString("#0.000")));
			}
			else
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_stages", (object)PlotFormsResources.NotFeasible);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveLabelFromMarker(IPlotobj oMyObj)
		{
			if (oMyObj.vUserData != null && int.TryParse(oMyObj.vUserData.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(_plotArea.FindObjByID(result));
				oMyObj.vUserData = 0;
				_plotArea.Refresh();
			}
		}

		private void SwapPlotType()
		{
			if (_plotArea.PlotType == PlotType.Equilateral)
			{
				_plotArea.PlotType = PlotType.RightAngled;
				SwitchMenuHeader = PlotFormsResources.SwitchToEquilateral;
			}
			else if (_plotArea.PlotType == PlotType.RightAngled)
			{
				_plotArea.PlotType = PlotType.Equilateral;
				SwitchMenuHeader = PlotFormsResources.SwitchToTriangle;
			}
			_plotArea.Refresh();
		}

		private void ZoomIn()
		{
			if (_plot.IsRegionDefined())
			{
				_plot.Zoom((short)4);
				double num = 0.0;
				double num2 = 0.0;
				_plot.GetAxisRange((short)0, ref num, ref num2);
				_plotArea.ZoomMode = num2 - num;
				Is_btnZoomIn_Command_Enabled = false;
				Is_btnZoomOut_Command_Enabled = true;
				Is_btnZoomFull_Command_Enabled = true;
			}
			IsStdZoomInCommandCanExecute = false;
			IsStdZoomOutCommandCanExecute = true;
			IsStdZoomFullCommandCanExecute = true;
		}

		private void ZoomOut(bool bFull = false)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double zoomMode = _plotArea.ZoomMode;
			_plot.Zoom((short)2);
			_plot.GetAxisRange((short)0, ref num, ref zoomMode);
			if (_plotArea.ZoomMode == 1.0 || bFull)
			{
				IsStdZoomOutCommandCanExecute = false;
				IsStdZoomFullCommandCanExecute = false;
				if (bFull)
				{
					_plot.Zoom((short)3);
					_plotArea.ZoomMode = 1.0;
				}
			}
		}

		private void TielineDeleteCommand_Execute(object args)
		{
			IPlotobj lastID = _plotArea.LastID;
			_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(lastID);
			if (lastID.eObjType == ObjType.TieLineVLLE || lastID.eObjType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE)
			{
				lastID = _plotArea.FindObjByID(lastID.iObjID + 1);
				_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(lastID);
				_plotArea.Highlight = false;
			}
			else if (lastID.eObjType == ObjType.MarkVLLE || lastID.eObjType == ObjType.MarkVLLEUser)
			{
				lastID = _plotArea.FindObjByID(lastID.iObjID - 1);
				_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(lastID);
				_plotArea.Highlight = false;
			}
		}

		private void TielinePropertiesCommand_Execute(object args)
		{
			ShowPropertyManager((PageEnum)3);
		}

		private void MarkerDeleteCommand_Execute(object args)
		{
			IPlotobj lastID = _plotArea.LastID;
			RemoveLabelFromMarker(lastID);
			_plotArea.CollectionRemoveObj(lastID);
			IsMarkerRemoveLabelCanExecute = false;
		}

		private void MarkerAddBoilingPointCommand_Execute(object args)
		{
			AddBoilingPoint(_plotArea.LastID.GetdbPointsArr(0, 0), _plotArea.LastID.GetdbPointsArr(0, 1));
			IsMarkerRemoveLabelCanExecute = true;
		}

		private void AddBoilingPoint(double sX, double sY)
		{
			double[] vComposition = new double[3]
			{
				sX,
				sY,
				1.0 - sX - sY
			};
			_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.GetBoilingPoint(vComposition, out var dbBoilingPoint);
			MarkerText(AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(dbBoilingPoint, _plotArea.PlotDisplayUnit).ToString("#0.0000") + _plotArea.PlotDisplayUnit);
		}

		private void MarkerText(string text)
		{
			//IL_00aa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				IPlotobj plotobj = new LabelObj();
				_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.TextUser);
				plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 2;
				for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
				{
					plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, i, _plotArea.LastID.GetdbPointsArr(0, i));
				}
				plotobj.vUserData = text;
				plotobj.AddObj();
				int iObjID = plotobj.iObjID;
				_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
				plotobj = _plotArea.FindObjByID(_plotArea.LastID.iObjID);
				plotobj.vUserData = iObjID;
				_plotArea.Refresh();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void MarkerAddTextCommand_Execute(object args)
		{
			if (MarkerAddText())
			{
				IsMarkerRemoveLabelCanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		private bool MarkerAddText()
		{
			AddMarkerText addMarkerText = new AddMarkerText();
			if (addMarkerText.ShowDialog().Value)
			{
				MarkerText(addMarkerText.MarkerText);
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void FinishedAdding()
		{
			if (_plotArea != null)
			{
				_plotArea.MousePointer(1);
				_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
				_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, true);
				_plotArea.AddManyTimes = false;
				IsBtnCurveChecked = false;
				IsBtnTieLineChecked = false;
				IsBtnMarkerChecked = false;
				IsBtnDrawLineChecked = false;
			}
		}

		public void CurveAdd_Start(bool bAddMany = false)
		{
			_plotArea.MousePointer(2);
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingCurvePoint;
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, false);
			_plotArea.AddManyTimes = bAddMany;
		}

		public void CurveAdd_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			//IL_00b9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0107: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			double num = 1.0 - (X + Y);
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3] { X, Y, num };
			MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
			if (_plotArea.BasisType != BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, _plotArea.BasisType, 3);
			}
			IPlotobj plotobj = null;
			object vDistillationCurve = null;
			int nDirection = 0;
			if (_objConsep.CurveType == Curve.Residue)
			{
				try
				{
					_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.GetResidueCurve(Composition_Array, out nDirection, out vDistillationCurve);
					plotobj = new CurveObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.CurveResidue);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.AddResidueCurveFailed, ((MMViewModel)this).GetProductName(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				try
				{
					_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.GetDistillationCurve(Composition_Array, out nDirection, out vDistillationCurve);
					plotobj = new CurveObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.CurveDistill);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.AddDistillCurveFailed, ((MMViewModel)this).GetProductName(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
					return;
				}
			}
			plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = (vDistillationCurve as double[,]).GetLength(0);
			for (int i = 0; i < (vDistillationCurve as double[,]).GetLength(0); i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
				{
					plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(i, j, (vDistillationCurve as double[,])[i, j]);
				}
			}
			plotobj.vUserData = (short)nDirection;
			plotobj.AddObj();
			_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
			if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
			{
				MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
				ChangeBasis();
			}
			_plotArea.Refresh();
			if (!_plotArea.AddManyTimes)
			{
				FinishedAdding();
				IsBtnCurveChecked = false;
			}
		}

		public void CurveAddValue_Start()
		{
			AddByValue addByValue = new AddByValue(_plotArea.BasisType, _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left));
			bool? flag = addByValue.ShowDialog();
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			if (flag.Value)
			{
				Composition_Array[0] = addByValue.CompVal0.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[1] = addByValue.CompVal1.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[2] = addByValue.CompVal2.GetValueOrDefault();
				MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
				if (addByValue.ABV_Basis != _plotArea.BasisType)
				{
					if (_plotArea.BasisType == BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					}
					else
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					}
				}
				CurveAdd_End(Composition_Array[MTriGlobals.CO1], Composition_Array[MTriGlobals.CO2]);
			}
			FinishedAdding();
		}

		public void DrawLine_Start()
		{
			_plotArea.MousePointer(2);
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLine;
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, false);
		}

		public void DrawLine_Do(short X, short Y)
		{
			MTriGlobals.LineStart.X = X;
			MTriGlobals.LineStart.Y = Y;
			_plotArea.PlotControlPtr.DragLine(MTriGlobals.LineStart.X, MTriGlobals.LineStart.Y, X, Y, (short)3);
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_DrawingLineEnd;
		}

		public void DrawLine_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			double[] Composition_Array2 = new double[3];
			double[,] array = new double[2, 3];
			_plotArea.PlotControlPtr.ConvertToPlot(MTriGlobals.LineStart.X, MTriGlobals.LineStart.Y, ref array[0, 0], ref array[0, 1]);
			array[0, 2] = 1.0 - (array[0, 0] + array[0, 1]);
			array[1, 0] = X;
			array[1, 1] = Y;
			array[1, 2] = 1.0 - (array[1, 0] + array[1, 1]);
			MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref array[0, 0], ref array[0, 1], ref array[0, 2]);
			MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref array[1, 0], ref array[1, 1], ref array[1, 2]);
			IPlotobj plotobj = new CurveObj();
			_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.FreeHandLine);
			plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 2;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				Composition_Array[i] = array[0, i];
				Composition_Array2[i] = array[1, i];
			}
			if (_plotArea.BasisType != BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, _plotArea.BasisType, 3);
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array2, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, _plotArea.BasisType, 3);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
			{
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, j, Composition_Array[j]);
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(1, j, Composition_Array2[j]);
			}
			plotobj.AddObj();
			_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
			_plotArea.Refresh();
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			_plotArea.MousePointer(1);
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, true);
			IsBtnDrawLineChecked = false;
		}

		public void MarkerAdd_Start()
		{
			_plotArea.MousePointer(2);
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingMarkerPoint;
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, false);
		}

		public void MarkerAdd_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3]
			{
				X,
				Y,
				1.0 - X - Y
			};
			double[] array = new double[3];
			IPlotobj plotobj = new MarkerObj();
			_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.MarkUserPoint);
			MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
			if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis != BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, MTriGlobals.TPBasis, 3);
			}
			plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 1;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, i, Composition_Array[i]);
				array[i] = Composition_Array[i];
			}
			plotobj.AddObj();
			_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
			_plotArea.Refresh();
			for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
			{
				plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, j, Composition_Array[j]);
			}
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
			_plotArea.MousePointer(1);
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, true);
			IsBtnMarkerChecked = false;
		}

		public void MarkerAddValue_Start()
		{
			AddByValue addByValue = new AddByValue(_plotArea.BasisType, _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left));
			bool? flag = addByValue.ShowDialog();
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			if (flag.Value)
			{
				Composition_Array[0] = addByValue.CompVal0.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[1] = addByValue.CompVal1.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[2] = addByValue.CompVal2.GetValueOrDefault();
				MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
				if (addByValue.ABV_Basis != _plotArea.BasisType)
				{
					if (_plotArea.BasisType == BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					}
					else
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
