		public void TieLineAdd_Start(bool bAddMany = false)
		{
			_plotArea.MousePointer(2);
			_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_SelectingTieLinePoint;
			_plot.SetEvent((short)2, true, false);
			_plotArea.AddManyTimes = bAddMany;
		}

		public void TieLineAdd_End(double X, double Y)
		{
			//IL_022c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			double num = 1.0 - (X + Y);
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3] { X, Y, num };
			if (X + Y + num > 1.0)
			{
				Console.Beep();
				if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
				{
					MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
					ChangeBasis();
				}
				_plotArea.Refresh();
				return;
			}
			MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
			if (_plotArea.BasisType != BasisType.BT_Mole)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, _plotArea.BasisType, 3);
			}
			object vX = null;
			object vX2 = null;
			object vY = null;
			ObjType objType = ObjType.TieLineUserLLE;
			try
			{
				if (_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10])
				{
					_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.GetNewTieLineVLLE(Composition_Array, out vX, out vX2, out vY);
				}
				else
				{
					if (!_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[9])
					{
						Console.Beep();
						if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
						{
							MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
							ChangeBasis();
						}
						_plotArea.Refresh();
						return;
					}
					_objConsep.m_IDistillationMap.GetNewTieLineLLE(Composition_Array, out vX, out vX2);
				}
				objType = ((!_objLogic.UsrSelectCalc[10]) ? ObjType.TieLineUserLLE : ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				IPlotobj plotobj = new CurveObj();
				_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, objType);
				plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 2;
				for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
				{
					plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, i, (vX as double[])[i]);
					plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(1, i, (vX2 as double[])[i]);
				}
				plotobj.AddObj();
				_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
				if (objType == ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE)
				{
					plotobj = new MarkerObj();
					_plotArea.InitObj(plotobj, ObjType.MarkVLLE);
					plotobj.iNumberOfPoints = 1;
					for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
					{
						plotobj.SetdbPointsArr(0, j, (vY as double[])[j]);
					}
					plotobj.AddObj();
					_plotArea.CollectionAddObj(plotobj);
				}
				if (MTriGlobals.TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
				{
					MTriGlobals.TPBasis = BasisType.BT_Mole;
					ChangeBasis();
				}
				_plotArea.Refresh();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.DrawTielineFailed, ((MMViewModel)this).GetProductName(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			if (!_plotArea.AddManyTimes)
			{
				FinishedAdding();
				IsBtnTieLineChecked = false;
			}
		}

		public void TieLineAddValue_Start()
		{
			AddByValue addByValue = new AddByValue(_plotArea.BasisType, _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Right), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Top), _plotArea.GetPlotLabelComp(PAxis.A_Left));
			bool? flag = addByValue.ShowDialog();
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			if (flag.Value)
			{
				Composition_Array[0] = addByValue.CompVal0.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[1] = addByValue.CompVal1.GetValueOrDefault();
				Composition_Array[2] = addByValue.CompVal2.GetValueOrDefault();
				MTriGlobals.SortCoords(ref Composition_Array[0], ref Composition_Array[1], ref Composition_Array[2]);
				if (addByValue.ABV_Basis != _plotArea.BasisType)
				{
					if (_plotArea.BasisType == BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					}
					else
					{
						AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_Out, ref Composition_Array, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					}
				}
				TieLineAdd_End(Composition_Array[MTriGlobals.CO1], Composition_Array[MTriGlobals.CO2]);
			}
			FinishedAdding();
		}

		public void UpdatePlotView(chkView index, bool state)
		{
			switch (index)
			{
			case chkView.CV_VLLE:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.EnvVLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.EnvLLE);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Azeotropes:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkAzeotrope);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Disbound:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.CurveDistillBound);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_TieLines:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_ResDisCurve:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.CurveResidue);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.CurveDistill);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Markers:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkUserPoint);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TextUser);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_VaporLine:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkVLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkVLLEUser);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Temperature:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TextTemperature);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_MatBalLine:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MatBalLine);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Decanter:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineDecanter);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_FeedLabel:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TextFeed);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Feed:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkFeed);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Products:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.MarkMBProd);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_ProdLables:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TextMBProd);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Rectify:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.Rectify);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Strip:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.Strip);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_RectifyMarkers:
				AzeoGUIGlobals.gRectifyMarkerState = state;
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.RectifyMarker);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_StripMarkers:
				AzeoGUIGlobals.gStripMarkerState = state;
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.StripMarker);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_Feasible:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.FeasibleStrip);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.FeasibleRetify);
				if (state)
				{
					DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.Rectify);
					DisplayItem(state: false, ObjType.Strip);
				}
				break;
			case chkView.CV_YTop:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.YTopMarker);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_YTopLabel:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.YTopLabel);
				break;
			case chkView.CV_IsovolCurve:
			case chkView.CV_ResidueOnly:
			case chkView.CV_LLE:
				break;
			}
		}

		public void DisplayItem(bool state, ObjType what)
		{
			if (what == ObjType.CurveResidue || what == ObjType.CurveDistill)
			{
				_plotArea.UserMode = DoingWhat.DW_BeingNormal;
				_plotArea.MousePointer(1);
			}
			_plotArea.DisplayObj(state, what);
			_plotArea.Redraw();
		}

		public void UpdatePlotToolBar(string key, bool state)
		{
			switch (key)
			{
			case "TieLine":
				Is_btnTieLine_Command_Enabled = state;
				IsStdAddTielineCommandCanExecute = state;
				break;
			case "TieLineValue":
				Is__btnTieLineValue_Command_Enabled = state;
				break;
			case "Curve":
				Is_btnCurve_Command_Enabled = state;
				IsStdAddCuveCommandCanExecute = state;
				break;
			case "CurveValue":
				Is_btnCurveValue_Command_Enabled = state;
				break;
			case "Marker":
				Is_btnMarker_Command_Enabled = state;
				IsStdAddMarkerCommandCanExecute = state;
				break;
			case "MarkerValue":
				Is_btnMarkerValue_Command_Enabled = state;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void UpdateViewTieLines(Envelope key, bool state)
		{
			switch (key)
			{
			case Envelope.env_VLLE:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineVLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE);
				break;
			case Envelope.env_LLE:
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineLLE);
				DisplayItem(state, ObjType.TieLineUserLLE);
				break;
			}
		}

	public class NumericHelper
	{
		private static string m_strDecimalSymbol = string.Empty;

		public static string DecimalSymbol
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_strDecimalSymbol))
				{
					m_strDecimalSymbol = GetSystemDecimalSymbol();
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_strDecimalSymbol))
					{
						m_strDecimalSymbol = ".";
					}
				}
				return m_strDecimalSymbol;
			}
		}

		private static string GetSystemDecimalSymbol()
		{
			return CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat.NumberDecimalSeparator;
		}
	}
	public class NumericTextBox : System.Windows.Controls.TextBox
	{
		protected override void OnPreviewTextInput(TextCompositionEventArgs e)
		{
			int selectionStart = base.SelectionStart;
			string text = base.Text;
			char num = e.Text[0];
			if (num == '-' && selectionStart != 0)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			if (num == NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0] && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && text.Contains(NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0]))
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnPreviewTextInput(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			bool num = (Keyboard.Modifiers & ModifierKeys.Shift) != 0;
			char c = NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0];
			if (!num)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (c == '.')
				{
					flag = e.Key == Key.OemPeriod;
				}
				if (c == ',')
				{
					flag = e.Key == Key.OemComma;
				}
				if (e.Key == Key.Subtract || e.Key == Key.Decimal || flag || e.Key == Key.OemMinus || (e.Key >= Key.NumPad0 && e.Key <= Key.NumPad9) || (e.Key >= Key.D0 && e.Key <= Key.D9) || e.Key == Key.Tab)
				{
					if (e.Key == Key.Decimal && NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0] != '.')
					{
						if (e.OriginalSource is IInputElement inputElement)
						{
							inputElement.RaiseEvent(new TextCompositionEventArgs(e.Device, new TextComposition(InputManager.Current, inputElement, NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol))
							{
								RoutedEvent = UIElement.TextInputEvent
							});
							e.Handled = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						e.Handled = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(e);
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(double), typeof(string))]
	public class StringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (value is double num)
			{
				if (num == -999999.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return num.ToString("g6", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			}
			return value.ToString();
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			try
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)value))
				{
					return -999999;
				}
				if (new string[4]
				{
					"-",
					"-0",
					"-0" + NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0],
					"-" + NumericHelper.DecimalSymbol[0]
				}.Contains((string)value))
				{
					return null;
				}
				double result = -999999.0;
				if (double.TryParse((string)value, out result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return -999999;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class PfsPlotTabPageViewModel : MMTabPageViewModel
	{
		private string _sPlotWizardProgId;

		private string _sPlotId = "Temp";

		private IHNode _mainNode;

		private AxPfsplot _plot;

		private HappLS _eventAttachedHappLS;

		private CxsCase _eventAttachedCase;

		private bool bInReinit;

		private const int HINT_KERNEL_UPDATE = 301;

		private const int HINT_THREADMODAL_BEGIN = 235;

		private const int HINT_THREADMODAL_ENDED = 236;

		private const int HINT_ASC_NEWEVENT = 303;

		private const int HINT_UPDATE_RUNSTEP = 212;

		private const int HINT_UPDATE_RENAMEBLOCK = 207;

		private const int HINT_UPDATE_FORMS_ONLY = 201;

		private const int HINT_UPDATE_STORE = 204;

		private const int WFRF_RUNREINIT = 109;

		private const int SIM_EVENT_REFRESH = 1005;

		private const int SIM_EVENT_UPDATE_STATUS = 1004;

		public IHNode MainNode
		{
			get
			{
				if (_mainNode == null && ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
					text = text.Replace("appmodelV8", "appModelV8");
					_mainNode = ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.InvokeAction("Top.appModelV8.Custom Actions", "HappNode", new object[1] { text }) as IHNode;
				}
				return _mainNode;
			}
		}

		public string PlotId
		{
			get
			{
				return _sPlotId;
			}
			set
			{
				_sPlotId = value;
			}
		}

		public AxPfsplot pfsPlot
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (_plot == null)
				{
					_plot = new AxPfsplot();
				}
				return _plot;
			}
		}

		public string PlotWizardProgId
		{
			get
			{
				if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider != null)
				{
					string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "type", (string)null));
						if (text.ToUpper().IndexOf("PROPERTIES.ANALYSIS") != -1)
						{
							_sPlotWizardProgId = "WpfPLANALYSIS.RibbonPlotWizardMgr";
						}
						else
						{
							_sPlotWizardProgId = "WpfPL" + text2.ToUpper() + ".RibbonPlotWizardMgr";
						}
					}
				}
				return _sPlotWizardProgId;
			}
		}

		private void EnsureAttachedEvent(CxsCase cxsCase)
		{
			if (_eventAttachedCase != cxsCase)
			{
				if (_eventAttachedCase != null)
				{
					_eventAttachedCase.Message -= cxsCase_Message;
				}
				_eventAttachedCase = cxsCase;
				if (_eventAttachedCase != null)
				{
					_eventAttachedCase.Message += cxsCase_Message;
				}
			}
		}

		public void InitHappMsg()
		{
			if (_mainNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			HappLS happLS = _mainNode.Application as HappLS;
			if (_eventAttachedHappLS != happLS)
			{
				if (_eventAttachedHappLS != null)
				{
					new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(IAPHappEvents_Event), "OnUpdateMessage").RemoveEventHandler(_eventAttachedHappLS, new IAPHappEvents_OnUpdateMessageEventHandler(happLs_OnUpdateMessage));
				}
				_eventAttachedHappLS = happLS;
				if (_eventAttachedHappLS != null)
				{
					new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(IAPHappEvents_Event), "OnUpdateMessage").AddEventHandler(happLS, new IAPHappEvents_OnUpdateMessageEventHandler(happLs_OnUpdateMessage));
				}
			}
		}

		public void RemoveSubscribedMsgs()
		{
			CxsCase val = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<CxsCase>(((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.Message -= cxsCase_Message;
			}
			if (_eventAttachedHappLS != null)
			{
				new ComAwareEventInfo(typeof(IAPHappEvents_Event), "OnUpdateMessage").RemoveEventHandler(_eventAttachedHappLS, new IAPHappEvents_OnUpdateMessageEventHandler(happLs_OnUpdateMessage));
			}
		}

		private void cxsCase_Message(object sender, CxsMessageEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0014: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0020: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			foreach (CxsMessage message in e.Messages)
			{
				CxsMessage current = message;
				int msg_code = 0;
				if ((int)((CxsMessage)(ref current)).MessageType == 13)
				{
					if (((CxsMessage)(ref current)).Number != 1005)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (((CxsMessage)(ref current)).MessageDataCount > 0)
					{
						msg_code = Convert.ToInt32(((CxsMessage)(ref current)).MessageData[0]);
					}
					ProcessMessage(msg_code, string.Empty);
				}
			}
		}

		private void happLs_OnUpdateMessage(int msg_code, string msg_hint, object msg_object, int source_handle)
		{
			ProcessMessage(msg_code, msg_hint);
		}

		private void ProcessMessage(int msg_code, string msg_hint)
		{
			if (bInReinit && msg_code != 109)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (msg_code <= 109)
			{
				if (msg_code != 103 && msg_code == 109)
				{
					bInReinit = false;
				}
				return;
			}
			switch (msg_code)
			{
			default:
				_ = 201;
				break;
			case 118:
				bInReinit = true;
				break;
			case 117:
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	[ProgId("PlotForms.PfsPlotTabPage")]

		private void PfsPlotTabPage_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_hasLoaded)
			{
				return;
			}
			plotHost.Child = (System.Windows.Forms.Control)(object)PfsPlotViewModel.pfsPlot;
			AxPfsplot axpfsPlot = PfsPlotViewModel.pfsPlot;
			if (axpfsPlot == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			((System.Windows.Forms.Control)(object)axpfsPlot).CreateControl();
			plotHost.PreviewKeyDown += delegate(object objSender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs objArgs)
			{
				if (objArgs.Key == Key.Up || objArgs.Key == Key.Down || objArgs.Key == Key.Left || objArgs.Key == Key.Right || objArgs.Key == Key.Apps)
				{
					objArgs.Handled = true;
					int num = 0;
					switch (objArgs.Key)
					{
					case Key.Left:
						num = 37;
						break;
					case Key.Up:
						num = 38;
						break;
					case Key.Right:
						num = 39;
						break;
					case Key.Down:
						num = 40;
						break;
					case Key.Apps:
						num = 93;
						break;
					}
					axpfsPlot.KeyDown(num, 1, 0);
				}
			};
			UpdatePlotContent();
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).EventAggregator != null)
			{
				((CompositePresentationEvent<SimulationReinitializedEventArgs>)(object)((MMViewModelContainer)this).EventAggregator.GetEvent<SimulationReinitializedEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<SimulationReinitializedEventArgs>)OnSimulationReinitialized);
				((CompositePresentationEvent<SimulationStatusChangedEventArgs>)(object)((MMViewModelContainer)this).EventAggregator.GetEvent<SimulationStatusChangedEvent>()).Subscribe((Action<SimulationStatusChangedEventArgs>)OnSimulationStatusChanged);
			}
			_hasLoaded = true;
		}

		{
			UpdatePlotContent();
		}

		private void OnSimulationStatusChanged(SimulationStatusChangedEventArgs e)

		}

		private void UpdatePlotContent()
		{
			AxPfsplot pfsPlot = PfsPlotViewModel.pfsPlot;
			if (pfsPlot == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject parent = ((FrameworkElement)this).Parent;
			MMTabItem val = (MMTabItem)(object)((parent is MMTabItem) ? parent : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			PfsPlotViewModel.PlotId = ((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Tag as string;
			IHNode mainNode = PfsPlotViewModel.MainNode;
			IRibbonPlotWizardMgr val2 = null;
			IRibbonPlotWizardType val3 = null;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PfsPlotViewModel.PlotWizardProgId))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				val2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IRibbonPlotResolver>(((MMViewModelContainer)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ResolveRibbonPlotWizardMgr(PfsPlotViewModel.PlotWizardProgId);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (val2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			CHWizardPlot cHWizardPlot = (CHWizardPlot)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("9E14C11F-6706-4067-8112-35CE9468F3A5")));
			object obj = cHWizardPlot;
			val2.SetContext((object)mainNode, ref obj);
			val2.GetPlotType(PfsPlotViewModel.PlotId, ref val3);
			if (val3 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			val3.Context((object)mainNode, ref obj, ref num);
			val3.UpdateWizardData();
			IHPlotVal plotVal = cHWizardPlot.PlotVal;
			CopyWizardDatatoPlotComponent(plotVal, pfsPlot);
			if (((object)val3).ToString() == "WpfPLConSep.Temperature")
			{
				string unitString = GetUnitString(mainNode.Application.Tree, obj as IHWizardPlot);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitString))
				{
					pfsPlot.Title = PlotFormsResources.TemperatureProfile;
					pfsPlot.ylabel = PlotFormsResources.Temperature;
					pfsPlot.SetLabel((short)1, PlotFormsResources.Temperature);
				}
				else
				{
					pfsPlot.Title = $"{PlotFormsResources.TemperatureProfile}({unitString})";
					pfsPlot.ylabel = $"{PlotFormsResources.Temperature} ({unitString})";
					pfsPlot.SetLabel((short)1, $"{PlotFormsResources.Temperature} ({unitString})");
				}
			}
			((System.Windows.Forms.Control)(object)pfsPlot).Refresh();
			InitializeContextualRibbonTabs(pfsPlot);
		}

		private void InitializeContextualRibbonTabs(AxPfsplot axpfsPlot)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).EventAggregator == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			RibbonTabsRequestingEventArgs e = new RibbonTabsRequestingEventArgs
			{
				Tag = axpfsPlot
			};
			((CompositePresentationEvent<RibbonTabsRequestingEventArgs>)(object)((MMViewModelContainer)this).EventAggregator.GetEvent<RibbonTabsRequestingEvent>()).Publish(e);
			if (e.ContextualRibbonTabs == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			((MMTabPage)this).ContextualRibbonTabs.Clear();
			foreach (RibbonTabItem contextualRibbonTab in e.ContextualRibbonTabs)
			{
				((MMTabPage)this).ContextualRibbonTabs.Add(contextualRibbonTab);
			}
		}

		public string GetUnitString(IHNode top, IHWizardPlot wizardPlot)
		{
			object force = null;
			string result = string.Empty;
			int num = wizardPlot.get_unitrow((object)null);
			int num2 = wizardPlot.get_unitcol((object)null);
			if (num > 0)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = top.Application.Tree.FindNode("Unit Table");
				string path = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, num - 1, force);
				IHNode iHNode2 = iHNode.FindNode(path);
				if (iHNode2 != null)
				{
					result = iHNode2.Elements.get_Label(0, num2 - 1, force);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void CopyWizardDatatoPlotComponent(IHPlotVal plotValue, AxPfsplot plot)
		{
			if (plotValue == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ICxsDataWatch obj = ((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel.CreateCxsWatch();
			plot.Clear();
			obj.AddWatch("Top.appModelV8.Settings.plottitlefont", "value");
			obj.AddWatch("Top.appModelV8.Settings.plotlabelfont", "value");
			obj.AddWatch("Top.appModelV8.Settings.plotscalefont", "value");
			obj.AddWatch("Top.appModelV8.Settings.plotlegendfont", "value");
			obj.AddWatch("Top.appModelV8.Settings.plottextfont", "value");
			int num = 0;
			object[] values = obj.Values;
			obj.Unload();
			if (values != null)
			{
				for (num = 0; num < values.Count(); num++)
				{
					if (values[num] != null && values[num].GetType() != typeof(DBNull))
					{
						SetFontHelper(plot, num, CxsConverter.ValueToString(values[num]));
					}
				}
			}
			plot.Flipped = plotValue.SwapAxises;
			plot.ReverseXAxis = plotValue.ReverseXAxis;
			plot.AddTimeStamp = plotValue.AddTimeStamp;
			plot.ShowLegend = plotValue.ShowLegend;
			plot.GridType = plotValue.GridType;
			plot.ZeroLine = plotValue.ShowZeroLine;
			if (plotValue.MarkerSize != 0)
			{
				plot.MarkerSize = plotValue.MarkerSize;
			}
			plot.SquarePlot = plotValue.SquarePlot;
			plot.DiagonalLine = plotValue.ShowDiagonalLine;
			plot.DisplayType = plotValue.LineStyle;
			plot.PlotType = (short)plotValue.PlotType;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(plotValue.TitleInt))
			{
				string titleInt = plotValue.TitleInt;
				int num2 = titleInt.IndexOf(":");
				if (num2 != -1)
				{
					plot.Title = titleInt.Substring(num2 + 1).Trim();
				}
				else
				{
					plot.Title = plotValue.TitleInt;
				}
			}
			plot.xlabel = plotValue.XAxisTitleInt;
			plot.ylabel = plotValue.get_YAxisTitleInt(Type.Missing);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(plotValue.XAxisTitleInt))
			{
				plot.xlabel = plotValue.XAxisTitleInt;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(plotValue.ZAxisTitleInt))
			{
				plot.SetLabel((short)2, plotValue.ZAxisTitleInt);
			}
			plot.Parametric = plotValue.Parametric;
			plot.MultiYaxis = true;
			if (!plotValue.Parametric)
			{
				plot.nxvariables = (short)plotValue.NXVariables;
				plot.nvariables = (short)plotValue.NVariables;
				for (num = 1; num < plotValue.NVariables; num++)
				{
					plot.SetXVariable((short)num, (short)((num > plotValue.NXVariables) ? plotValue.NXVariables : num));
				}
				for (num = 0; num < plotValue.NVariables; num++)
				{
					string text = plotValue.get_LabelInt(num);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						plot.SetLegend((short)(num + 1), text);
					}
				}
				try
				{
					if (plotValue.get_AxisScale(0) != 0)
					{
						plot.SetScale((short)0, (short)plotValue.get_AxisScale(0));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				for (num = 0; num < plotValue.NVariables; num++)
				{
					plot.SetAxisMap((short)num, (short)(plotValue.get_AxisMap(num) + 1));
					int num3 = plotValue.get_CurveStyle(num);
					if ((num3 & 0xFF) != 0)
					{
						short num4 = 0;
						short num5 = 0;
						short num6 = 0;
						plot.GetAttribute((short)num, ref num4, ref num5, ref num6);
						if ((num3 & 0xF) != 0)
						{
							num4 = -1;
						}
						if ((num3 & 0xF0) != 0)
						{
							num5 = 0;
						}
						plot.SetAttribute((short)num, num4, num5, num6);
					}
					try
					{
						if (plotValue.get_AxisScale(num + 1) != 0)
						{
							plot.SetScale((short)(num + 1), (short)plotValue.get_AxisScale(num + 1));
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			LoadPlotValues(plot, plotValue);
		}

		public static void LoadPlotValues(AxPfsplot _plot, IHPlotVal _plotValue)
		{
			//IL_0013: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (_plotValue.Parametric)
			{
				int nPoints = _plotValue.NPoints;
				ParametricData val = new ParametricData();
				for (int i = 0; i < nPoints; i++)
				{
					val.AddData((long)i, _plotValue.get_YDataValue(0, i));
				}
				long nCurve = val.GetNCurve();
				int nXVariables = _plotValue.NXVariables;
				if (nCurve > 0)
				{
					_plot.nxvariables = (short)(nCurve * nXVariables);
				}
				else
				{
					_plot.nxvariables = (short)nXVariables;
				}
				int nVariables = _plotValue.NVariables;
				if (nVariables > 1)
				{
					if (nCurve > 0)
					{
						_plot.nvariables = (short)((nVariables - 1) * nCurve);
					}
					else
					{
						_plot.nvariables = (short)(nVariables - 1);
					}
				}
				for (int j = 1; j <= (nVariables - 1) * nCurve; j++)
				{
					_plot.SetXVariable((short)j, (short)((j - 1) % (nXVariables * nCurve) + 1));
				}
				string newValue = default(string);
				for (int k = 1; k < nVariables; k++)
				{
					string text = _plotValue.get_LabelInt(k);
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num = text.IndexOf("%param%");
					for (int l = 0; l < nCurve; l++)
					{
						if (double.IsNaN(val.GetParValue((long)l)) || val.GetParValue((long)l) == HelperFuncs.DNan())
						{
							newValue = null;
						}
						else
						{
							HelperFuncs.HelperFormatParamValue(val.GetParValue((long)l), ref newValue);
						}
						if (num != -1)
						{
							string text2 = text.Replace("%param%", newValue);
							_plot.SetLegend((short)((k - 1) * nCurve + l + 1), text2);
						}
						else
						{
							_plot.SetLegend((short)((k - 1) * nCurve + l + 1), text);
						}
					}
				}
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				for (int m = 1; m < nVariables; m++)
				{
					num2 = _plotValue.get_AxisMap(m);
					for (int n = 0; n < nCurve; n++)
					{
						_plot.SetAxisMap((short)((m - 1) * nCurve + n), (short)(num2 + 1));
					}
					num3 = _plotValue.get_CurveStyle(m);
					if ((num3 & 0xF) != 15 && (num3 & 0xF0) != 240)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int num4 = 0; num4 < nCurve; num4++)
					{
						short num5 = 0;
						short num6 = 0;
						short num7 = 0;
						_plot.GetAttribute((short)((m - 1) * nCurve + num4), ref num5, ref num6, ref num7);
						if ((num3 & 0xF) == 15)
						{
							num5 = -1;
						}
						if ((num3 & 0xF0) == 240)
						{
							num6 = 0;
						}
						_plot.SetAttribute((short)((m - 1) * nCurve + num4), num5, num6, num7);
					}
				}
				_plot.npoints = (short)nPoints;
				for (int num8 = 0; num8 < nPoints; num8++)
				{
					double num9 = 1E+28;
					for (int num10 = 0; num10 < nXVariables * nCurve; num10++)
					{
						_plot.SetXData((short)(num10 + 1), (short)num8, num9);
					}
					for (int num11 = 0; num11 < (nVariables - 1) * nCurve; num11++)
					{
						_plot.SetData((short)(num11 + 1), (short)num8, num9);
					}
					for (int num12 = 0; num12 < nXVariables; num12++)
					{
						double num13 = _plotValue.get_XDataValue(num12, num8);
						if (double.IsNaN(num13) || num13 == HelperFuncs.DNan())
						{
							num13 = num9;
						}
						_plot.SetXData((short)(num12 * nCurve + val.GetCurve((long)num8) + 1), (short)num8, num13);
					}
					for (int num14 = 1; num14 < nVariables; num14++)
					{
						double num15 = _plotValue.get_YDataValue(num14, num8);
						if (double.IsNaN(num15) || num15 == HelperFuncs.DNan())
						{
							num15 = num9;
						}
						_plot.SetData((short)((num14 - 1) * nCurve + val.GetCurve((long)num8) + 1), (short)num8, num15);
					}
				}
				bool flag = true;
				for (int num16 = 0; num16 < nXVariables; num16++)
				{
					if (!_plotValue.get_XDataInteger(num16))
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_plot.SetInteger((short)0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int nPoints2 = _plotValue.NPoints;
				int nXVariables2 = _plotValue.NXVariables;
				int nVariables2 = _plotValue.NVariables;
				_plot.npoints = (short)nPoints2;
				_plot.nxvariables = (short)nXVariables2;

				{

					{
						double num20 = _plotValue.get_XDataValue(num19, num18);
						if (double.IsNaN(num20) || num20 == HelperFuncs.DNan())
						{
							num20 = num17;
						}
						_plot.SetXData((short)(num19 + 1), (short)num18, num20);
					}
					for (int num21 = 0; num21 < nVariables2; num21++)
					{
						double num22 = _plotValue.get_YDataValue(num21, num18);
						if (double.IsNaN(num22) || num22 == HelperFuncs.DNan())
						{

					}
				}
				bool flag2 = true;
				for (int num23 = 0; num23 < nXVariables2; num23++)
				{
					if (!_plotValue.get_XDataInteger(num23))
					{
						flag2 = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					_plot.SetInteger((short)0);
				}
			}
			long num24 = _plotValue.AnnotationCount;
			for (int num25 = 0; num25 < num24; num25++)
			{
				PLOTTEXT pLOTTEXT = _plotValue.get_AnnotationText((int)(short)num25);
				string text3 = pLOTTEXT.text;
				string text4 = pLOTTEXT.fontname;
				_plot.AddText(text3, (float)pLOTTEXT.x, (float)pLOTTEXT.y, (short)pLOTTEXT.color, pLOTTEXT.alignment, text4, pLOTTEXT.size, pLOTTEXT.style);
			}
			string text5 = "Y AXIS";
			int num26 = 0;
			try
			{
				while (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
				{

					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
					{

					}
					num26++;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public static void SetFontHelper(AxPfsplot _plot, int fontId, string setting)
		{
			setting = setting.TrimStart();
			string[] array = setting.Split(' ');
			int result = 0;
			if (int.TryParse(array[0], out result))
			{
				int num = 0;
				string text = null;
				string text2 = setting;
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = text2.IndexOf(array[0]);
				if (num3 == 0)
				{
					num2 = array[0].Length;
					text2 = text2.Remove(num3, num2);
				}
				text2 = text2.TrimStart();
				num3 = text2.IndexOf("pt. ");
				if (num3 == 0)
				{
					num2 = "pt. ".Length;
					text2 = text2.Remove(num3, num2);
				}
				num3 = text2.IndexOf(" Bold");
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					num |= 2;
					num2 = " Bold".Length;
					text2 = text2.Remove(num3, num2);
				}
				num3 = text2.IndexOf(" Italic");
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					num |= 1;
					num2 = " Italic".Length;
					text2 = text2.Remove(num3, num2);
				}
				num3 = text2.IndexOf(" Underline");
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					num |= 4;
					num2 = " Underline".Length;
					text2 = text2.Remove(num3, num2);
				}
				text = text2;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					_plot.SetFont((short)fontId, text, (short)result, (short)num);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool _isCVResDisCurveEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVDecanterEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVMatBalLineEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVFeedEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVProductsEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVProdLabelsEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVRectifyEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVStripEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVStripMarkersEnabled = true;

		private bool _IsCVYTopEnabled = true;

		private bool _IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVFeasibleEnabled = true;

		private bool _isCVMarkersChecked = true;

		private bool _isEnvelopeChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVTieLinesChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVVaporLinesChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVAzeotropesChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVTemperatureChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVDisboundChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVResDisCurveChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVDecanterChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVMatBalLineChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVFeedChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVFeedLabelsChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVProductsChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVProdLabelsChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVRectifyChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVStripChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVRectifyMarkersChecked = true;

		private bool _isCVStripMarkersChecked = true;

		private bool _IsCVYTopChecked = true;

		private bool _IsCVYTopLabelChecked = true;

		private bool _IsCVFeasibleChecked;

		private ICommand _okCommand;

		private ICommand _cancelCommand;

		public bool IsEnvelopeChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _isEnvelopeChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_isEnvelopeChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsEnvelopeChecked");
				if (value)
				{
					IsCVTieLinesEnabled = true;
					if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[10])
					{
						IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = true;
						return;
					}
					IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
					IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVTieLinesEnabled = false;
					IsCVTieLinesChecked = false;
					IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
					IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsCVTieLinesChecked

			get
			{
				return _isCVTieLinesChecked;

		public bool IsCVFeasibleChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return _IsCVFeasibleChecked;
			}
			set
			{
				_IsCVFeasibleChecked = value;
				OnPropertyChanged("IsCVFeasibleChecked");
				if (!value)
				{
					IsCVStripEnabled = true;
					IsCVRectifyEnabled = true;
					return;
				}
				IsCVStripEnabled = false;
				IsCVRectifyEnabled = false;
				IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = false;
				IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
				IsCVStripChecked = false;
				IsCVRectifyChecked = false;
				IsCVStripMarkersChecked = false;
				IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
			}
		}

		public ICommand OKCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_okCommand == null)
				{
					_okCommand = (ICommand)new DelegateCommand<object>((Action<object>)OKCommand_Executed);
				}
				return _okCommand;
			}
		}

		public ICommand CancelCommand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cancelCommand == null)
				{
					_cancelCommand = (ICommand)new DelegateCommand<object>((Action<object>)CancelCommand_Executed);
				}
				return _cancelCommand;
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public ShowHideDlgViewModel(MMConSep_TernaryPlot_ViewModel plotViewModel)
		{
			_plotViewModel = plotViewModel;
			InitializeState();
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVMarkersChecked"))
			{
				IsCVMarkersChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVMarkersChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsEnvelopeChecked"))
			{
				IsEnvelopeChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsEnvelopeChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVTieLinesChecked"))
			{
				IsCVTieLinesChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVTieLinesChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVVaporLinesChecked"))
			{
				IsCVVaporLinesChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVVaporLinesChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVAzeotropesChecked"))
			{
				IsCVAzeotropesChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVAzeotropesChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVTemperatureChecked"))
			{
				IsCVTemperatureChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVTemperatureChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVDisboundChecked"))
			{
				IsCVDisboundChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVDisboundChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVResDisCurveChecked"))
			{
				IsCVResDisCurveChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVResDisCurveChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVDecanterChecked"))
			{
				IsCVDecanterChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVDecanterChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVMatBalLineChecked"))
			{
				IsCVMatBalLineChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVMatBalLineChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVFeedChecked"))
			{
				IsCVFeedChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeedChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVFeedLabelsChecked"))
			{
				IsCVFeedLabelsChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeedLabelsChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVProductsChecked"))
			{
				IsCVProductsChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVProductsChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVProdLabelsChecked"))
			{
				IsCVProdLabelsChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVProdLabelsChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVRectifyChecked"))
			{
				IsCVRectifyChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVRectifyChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVStripChecked"))
			{
				IsCVStripChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVStripChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked"))
			{
				IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVStripMarkersChecked"))
			{
				IsCVStripMarkersChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVStripMarkersChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVYTopChecked"))
			{
				IsCVYTopChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVYTopChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVYTopLabelChecked"))
			{
				IsCVYTopLabelChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVYTopLabelChecked"];
			}
			if (StoredShowHideData.ContainsKey("IsCVFeasibleChecked"))
			{
				IsCVFeasibleChecked = StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeasibleChecked"];
			}
		}

		public void OnPropertyChanged(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		public void InitializeState()
		{
			if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[9] || _plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[10])
			{
				if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_vXCurve == null || (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_vXCurve as Array).Length == 0)
				{
					IsEnvelopeEnabled = false;
					IsCVTieLinesEnabled = false;
					IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
					IsEnvelopeChecked = false;
					IsCVTieLinesChecked = false;
					IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
				}
				else
				{
					IsEnvelopeEnabled = true;
					if (IsEnvelopeChecked)
					{
						IsCVTieLinesEnabled = true;
						if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[10])
						{
							IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = true;
						}
						else
						{
							IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
							IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						IsCVTieLinesEnabled = false;
						IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
						IsCVTieLinesChecked = false;
						IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsEnvelopeEnabled = false;
				IsCVTieLinesEnabled = false;
				IsCVVaporLinesEnabled = false;
				IsEnvelopeChecked = false;
				IsCVTieLinesChecked = false;
				IsCVVaporLinesChecked = false;
			}
			if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[0])
			{
				IsCVAzeotropesEnabled = true;
				if (IsCVAzeotropesChecked)
				{
					IsCVTemperatureEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVTemperatureEnabled = false;
					IsCVTemperatureChecked = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsCVAzeotropesEnabled = false;
				IsCVAzeotropesChecked = false;
				IsCVTemperatureEnabled = false;
				IsCVTemperatureChecked = false;
			}
			if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[1])
			{
				IsCVDisboundEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				IsCVDisboundEnabled = false;
				IsCVDisboundChecked = false;
			}
			if (_plotViewModel._objLogic.UsrSelected[3])
			{
				IsCVResDisCurveEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				IsCVResDisCurveEnabled = false;
				IsCVResDisCurveChecked = false;
			}
			if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_nMatTot != 3)
			{
				IsCVDecanterEnabled = false;
				IsCVDecanterChecked = false;
				IsCVMatBalLineEnabled = false;
				IsCVMatBalLineChecked = false;
				IsCVFeedEnabled = false;
				IsCVFeedChecked = false;
				IsCVProductsEnabled = false;
				IsCVProductsChecked = false;
				IsCVProdLabelsEnabled = false;
				IsCVProdLabelsChecked = false;
			}
			else
			{
				IsCVDecanterEnabled = true;
				IsCVMatBalLineEnabled = true;
				IsCVFeedEnabled = true;
				IsCVProductsEnabled = true;
				if (IsCVProductsChecked)
				{
					IsCVProdLabelsEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVProdLabelsEnabled = false;
					IsCVProdLabelsChecked = false;
				}
				if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_nPhase == 3 && _plotViewModel._objConsep.m_Decanter == Configuration.Decanter)
				{
					IsCVDecanterEnabled = true;
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVDecanterEnabled = false;
					IsCVDecanterChecked = false;
				}
			}
			if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_nMode == 1)
			{
				if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_vx_rect != null && (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_vx_rect as Array).Length > 0)
				{
					IsCVRectifyEnabled = true;
					IsCVStripEnabled = true;
					IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = true;
					IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = true;
					if (!IsCVRectifyChecked)
					{
						IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
						IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
					}
					if (!IsCVStripChecked)
					{
						IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
						IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVRectifyEnabled = false;
					IsCVStripEnabled = false;
					IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
					IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = false;
					IsCVRectifyChecked = false;
					IsCVStripChecked = false;
					IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
					IsCVStripMarkersChecked = false;
				}
				if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_Decanter == Configuration.Decanter)
				{
					IsCVYTopEnabled = true;
					if (IsCVYTopChecked)
					{
						IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = true;
					}
					else
					{
						IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = false;
						IsCVYTopLabelChecked = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVYTopEnabled = false;
					IsCVYTopChecked = false;
					IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = false;
					IsCVYTopLabelChecked = false;
				}
				if (_plotViewModel._objConsep.m_nfeasible == 1)
				{
					IsCVFeasibleEnabled = true;
					if (IsCVFeasibleChecked)
					{
						IsCVRectifyEnabled = false;
						IsCVStripEnabled = false;
						IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
						IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = false;
						IsCVRectifyChecked = false;
						IsCVStripChecked = false;
						IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
						IsCVStripMarkersChecked = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					IsCVFeasibleEnabled = false;
					IsCVFeasibleChecked = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				IsCVRectifyEnabled = false;
				IsCVStripEnabled = false;
				IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = false;
				IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = false;
				IsCVFeasibleEnabled = false;
				IsCVYTopEnabled = false;
				IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = false;
				IsCVRectifyChecked = false;
				IsCVStripChecked = false;
				IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = false;
				IsCVStripMarkersChecked = false;
				IsCVFeasibleChecked = false;
				IsCVYTopChecked = false;
				IsCVYTopLabelChecked = false;
			}
		}

		private void OKCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			ApplyShowHideSettings();
			if (args is Window window)
			{
				window.DialogResult = true;
				window.Hide();
			}
		}

		private void ApplyShowHideSettings()
		{
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Markers, IsCVMarkersChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("Marker", IsCVMarkersChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("MarkerValue", IsCVMarkersChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVMarkersChecked"] = IsCVMarkersChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_VLLE, IsEnvelopeChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_LLE, IsEnvelopeChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsEnvelopeChecked"] = IsEnvelopeChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("TieLine", IsCVTieLinesChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("TieLineValue", IsCVTieLinesChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdateViewTieLines(Envelope.env_VLLE, _isEnvelopeChecked && IsCVTieLinesChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdateViewTieLines(Envelope.env_LLE, _isEnvelopeChecked && IsCVTieLinesChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVTieLinesChecked"] = IsCVTieLinesChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_VaporLine, IsCVVaporLinesChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVVaporLinesChecked"] = IsCVVaporLinesChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Azeotropes, IsCVAzeotropesChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVAzeotropesChecked"] = IsCVAzeotropesChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Temperature, IsCVTemperatureChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVTemperatureChecked"] = IsCVTemperatureChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Disbound, IsCVDisboundChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVDisboundChecked"] = IsCVDisboundChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_ResDisCurve, IsCVResDisCurveChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("Curve", IsCVResDisCurveChecked);
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotToolBar("CurveValue", IsCVResDisCurveChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVResDisCurveChecked"] = IsCVResDisCurveChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Decanter, IsCVDecanterChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVDecanterChecked"] = IsCVDecanterChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_MatBalLine, IsCVMatBalLineChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVMatBalLineChecked"] = IsCVMatBalLineChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Feed, IsCVFeedChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeedChecked"] = IsCVFeedChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_FeedLabel, IsCVFeedLabelsChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeedLabelsChecked"] = IsCVFeedLabelsChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Products, IsCVProductsChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVProductsChecked"] = IsCVProductsChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_ProdLables, IsCVProdLabelsChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVProdLabelsChecked"] = IsCVProdLabelsChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Rectify, IsCVRectifyChecked);
			IsCVRectifyMarkersEnabled = IsCVRectifyChecked;
			IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked = IsCVRectifyChecked;
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVRectifyChecked"] = IsCVRectifyChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Strip, IsCVStripChecked);
			IsCVStripMarkersEnabled = IsCVStripChecked;
			IsCVStripMarkersChecked = IsCVStripChecked;
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVStripChecked"] = IsCVStripChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_RectifyMarkers, IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked"] = IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_StripMarkers, IsCVStripMarkersChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVStripMarkersChecked"] = IsCVStripMarkersChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_YTop, IsCVYTopChecked);
			IsCVYTopLabelEnabled = IsCVYTopChecked;
			IsCVYTopLabelChecked = IsCVYTopChecked;
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVYTopChecked"] = IsCVYTopChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_YTopLabel, IsCVYTopLabelChecked);
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVYTopLabelChecked"] = IsCVYTopLabelChecked;
			_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Feasible, IsCVFeasibleChecked);
			if (!IsCVFeasibleChecked)
			{
				_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Strip, IsCVStripChecked);
				_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_Rectify, IsCVRectifyChecked);
				_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_StripMarkers, IsCVStripMarkersChecked);
				_plotViewModel.UpdatePlotView(chkView.CV_RectifyMarkers, IsCVRectifyMarkersChecked);
			}
			StoredShowHideData["IsCVFeasibleChecked"] = IsCVFeasibleChecked;
		}

		private void CancelCommand_Executed(object args)
		{
			if (args is Window window)
			{
				window.DialogResult = false;
				window.Hide();
			}
		}
	}

		public static double CEnergyFlowRate(double dbEnergy, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbEnergy;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "BTU/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 0.29307107;
				break;
			case "CAL/S":
				dbEnergy /= 4.1868;
				break;
			case "GJ/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 277777.78;
				break;
			case "KCAL/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 1.1629833;
				break;
			case "MMKCAL/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 1162983.3;
				break;
			case "MMBTU/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 293071.07;
				break;
			case "MMKCAL/DAY":
				dbEnergy /= 48457.6375;
				break;
			case "MMKBTU/DAY":
				dbEnergy /= 12211.29458;
				break;
			case "PCU/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 0.527527925;
				break;
			case "MMPCU/HR":
				dbEnergy /= 527527.925;
				break;
			case "KJ/S":
				dbEnergy /= 1000.0;
				break;
			case "KW":
				dbEnergy /= 1000.0;
				break;
			}
			return dbEnergy;
		}

		public static double CEnergy(double dbEnergy, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbEnergy;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "BTU":
				dbEnergy *= 0.001055056;
				break;
			case "J":
				dbEnergy *= 1.0;
				break;
			case "KJ":
				dbEnergy *= 0.001;
				break;
			case "CAL":
				dbEnergy *= 4.184;
				break;
			case "MMBTU":
				dbEnergy *= 1.05506E-09;
				break;
			case "GJ":
				dbEnergy *= 1E-09;
				break;
			case "KCAL":
				dbEnergy *= 0.004184;
				break;
			case "MJ":
				dbEnergy *= 1E-06;
				break;
			}
			return dbEnergy;
		}

		public static double CTemp(double dbTemp, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbTemp;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "C":
				dbTemp = -273.15 + dbTemp;
				break;
			case "F":
				dbTemp = -459.67 + 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			case "R":
				dbTemp = 1.8 * dbTemp;
				break;
			}
			return dbTemp;
		}

		public static double KTemp(double dbTemp, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbTemp;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "C":
				dbTemp = 273.15 + dbTemp;
				break;
			case "F":
				dbTemp = (459.67 + dbTemp) / 1.8;
				break;
			case "R":
				dbTemp /= 1.8;
				break;
			}
			return dbTemp;
		}

		public static double CPressure(double dbPress, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbPress;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "PSI":
				dbPress /= 6894.75729316836;
				break;
			case "PSIA":
				dbPress /= 6894.75729316836;
				break;
			case "ATM":
				dbPress /= 101325.0;
				break;
			case "LBF/SQFT":
				dbPress /= 47.880259;
				break;
			case "BAR":
				dbPress /= 100000.0;
				break;
			case "BARA":
				dbPress /= 100000.0;
				break;
			case "MMHG":
				dbPress /= 133.3223684;
				break;
			case "TORR":
				dbPress /= 133.3223684;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER":
				dbPress /= 249.0889;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F":
				dbPress /= 248.84;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCM":
				dbPress /= 98066.5;
				break;
			case "KPA":
				dbPress /= 1000.0;
				break;
			case "MPA":
				dbPress /= 1000000.0;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER":
				dbPress /= 9.80665;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-60F":
				dbPress /= 9.79685039370079;
				break;
			case "MBAR":
				dbPress /= 100.0;
				break;
			case "LB/FT-SQSEC":
				dbPress /= 1.4881662;
				break;
			case "IN-HG":
				dbPress /= 3386.38815789474;
				break;
			case "ATMG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 101325.0;
				break;
			case "BARG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 100000.0;
				break;
			case "IN-HG-VAC":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / -3386.38815789474;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F-G":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 248.84;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F-VAC":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / -248.84;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-G":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 249.0889;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-VAC":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / -249.0889;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-G":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 9.80665;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-60F-G":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 9.79685039370079;
				break;
			case "MMHG-VAC":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / -133.3223684;
				break;
			case "MPAG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 1000000.0;
				break;
			case "KPAG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 1000.0;
				break;
			case "PAG":
				dbPress -= gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "PSIG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 6894.7573;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCMG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 98066.5;
				break;
			case "MBARG":
				dbPress = (dbPress - gAmbientPressure) / 100.0;
				break;
			}
			return dbPress;
		}

		public static double KPressure(double dbPress, string unit)
		{
			if (unit == null)
			{
				return dbPress;
			}
			switch (unit.ToUpper())
			{
			case "PSIA":
			case "PSI":
				dbPress *= 6894.75729316836;
				break;
			case "ATM":
				dbPress *= 101325.0;
				break;
			case "LBF/SQFT":
				dbPress *= 47.880259;
				break;
			case "BARA":
			case "BAR":
				dbPress *= 100000.0;
				break;
			case "MMHG":
			case "TORR":
			case "MMHG, TORR":
				dbPress *= 133.3223684;
				break;
			case "IN-HG":
				dbPress *= 3386.38815789474;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER":
				dbPress *= 249.0889;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F":
				dbPress *= 248.84;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCM":
				dbPress *= 98066.5;
				break;
			case "KPA":
				dbPress *= 1000.0;
				break;
			case "MPA":
				dbPress *= 1000000.0;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER":
				dbPress *= 9.80665;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-60F":
				dbPress *= 9.79685039370079;
				break;
			case "MBAR":
				dbPress *= 100.0;
				break;
			case "LB/FT-SQSEC":
				dbPress *= 1.4881662;
				break;
			case "ATMG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 101325.0 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "BARG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 100000.0 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "IN-HG-VAC":
				dbPress = dbPress * -3386.38815789474 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F-G":
				dbPress = dbPress * 248.84 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-60F-VAC":
				dbPress = dbPress * -248.84 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-G":
				dbPress = dbPress * 249.0889 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "IN-WATER-VAC":
				dbPress = dbPress * -249.0889 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-G":
				dbPress = dbPress * 9.80665 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "MM-WATER-60F-G":
				dbPress = dbPress * 9.79685039370079 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "MMHG-VAC":
				dbPress = dbPress * -133.3223684 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "MPAG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 1000000.0 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "KPAG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 1000.0 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "PAG":
				dbPress += gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "PSIG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 6894.7573 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "KG/SQCMG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 98066.5 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			case "MBARG":
				dbPress = dbPress * 100.0 + gAmbientPressure;
				break;
			}
			return dbPress;
		}

		public static void ConvertBasis(Way dir, ref double[] Composition_Array, ref double[] MolecWtArray, BasisType Baz, int nComp)
		{
			if (nComp > MolecWtArray.Length || nComp > Composition_Array.Length)
			{
				throw new IndexOutOfRangeException();
			}
			try
			{
				if (dir == Way.W_In)
				{
					if (Baz != BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						return;
					}
					double num = 0.0;
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						num += Composition_Array[num2] * MolecWtArray[num2];
					}
					if (num != 0.0)
					{
						for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
						{
							Composition_Array[num2] = Composition_Array[num2] * MolecWtArray[num2] / num;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (Baz != BasisType.BT_Mass)
					{
						return;
					}
					double num = 0.0;
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						num += Composition_Array[num2] / MolecWtArray[num2];
					}
					if (num == 0.0)
					{
						return;
					}
					for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < nComp; num2++)
					{
						if (MolecWtArray[num2] != 0.0)
						{
							Composition_Array[num2] = Composition_Array[num2] / MolecWtArray[num2] / num;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private static double[] ChangeToMole(double[] CompostionArray, double[] MolarWghtArray)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				num += CompostionArray[num2] / MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				CompostionArray[num2] = CompostionArray[num2] * num / MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			return CompostionArray;
		}

		public static double[] ChangeToMass(double[] CompostionArray, double[] MolarWghtArray)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				num += CompostionArray[num2] * MolarWghtArray[num2];
			}
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 < CompostionArray.Length; num2++)
			{
				CompostionArray[num2] = CompostionArray[num2] * MolarWghtArray[num2] / num;
			}
			return CompostionArray;
		}

		public static double ChangeFlowBasis(double dbflow, double[] MolarWght, double[] MoleFraction)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			for (long num2 = 0L; num2 <= 2; num2++)
			{
				num += dbflow * MoleFraction[num2] * MolarWght[num2];
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static double CMassFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "KG/HR":
				result = dbflow * 3600.0;
				break;
			case "MTO/DAY":
				result = dbflow * 86.4;
				break;
			case "MTO/YEAR":
				result = dbflow * 31536.0;
				break;
			case "LB/S":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046;
				break;
			case "LB/HR":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046 * 3600.0;
				break;
			case "KG/MIN":
				result = dbflow * 60.0;
				break;
			case "LB/MIN":
				result = dbflow * 2.2046 * 60.0;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double KMassFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "KG/HR":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "MTO/DAY":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;
			case "MTO/YEAR":
				result = dbflow / 31536.0;
				break;
			case "LB/S":
				result = dbflow / 2.2046;
				break;
			case "LB/HR":
				result = dbflow / 7936.56;
				break;
			case "KG/MIN":
				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "LB/MIN":
				result = dbflow / 132.276;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double KMoleFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "kmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "kmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "tmol/day":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;
			case "lbmol/s":
				result = dbflow / 2.204;
				break;
			case "lbmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 132.28;
				break;
			case "lbmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 7936.6;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double CMoleFlowRate(double dbflow, string flowunit)
		{
			double result = dbflow;
			switch (flowunit)
			{
			case "kmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 60.0;
				break;
			case "kmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 3600.0;
				break;
			case "tmol/day":
				result = dbflow / 86.4;
				break;
			case "lbmol/s":
				result = dbflow / 2.204;
				break;
			case "lbmol/min":
				result = dbflow / 132.28;
				break;
			case "lbmol/hr":
				result = dbflow / 7936.6;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static void GetTempDir()
		{
			string environmentVariable = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TMP");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(environmentVariable))
			{
				TempFilePath = environmentVariable;
			}
		}

		public static void TraceToFile(string Msg, string fName, bool NewFile)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(TempFilePath))
			{
				GetTempDir();
			}
		}

		public static string GetSplitSystemBin()
		{
			return ReadWriteEasyReg.GetSplitDir();
		}

			for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++)

				m_bDisplay[i] = true;
			}

		public double GetMoleWt(string sComp)
		{
			if (sComp.EndsWith("\0"))
			{
				sComp = sComp.Substring(0, sComp.Length - 1);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < m_szComp.Length; i++)
			{
				if (m_szComp[i].Trim().Equals(sComp))
				{
					return m_vMole[i];
				}
			}
			return 0.0;
		}

		public void Calculate_Azeotropes()
		{
			m_IDistillationMap.GetAzeotropes(out m_nAzeotropes, out m_vAzeotropes, out m_vAzeoTemps, out m_vAzeoPhase, out m_vAzeoStability);
		}

		public void Calculate_DistBoundry()
		{
			m_IDistillationMap.GetDistillationBoundary(out m_nCurvePoints, out m_vCurveDir, out m_vCurve);
		}

		public void Calculate_VLLEenvelope()
		{
			//IL_0056: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				double dbPlaitTemp = 0.0;
				m_IDistillationMap.ComputeEnvelopeVLLE();
				m_IDistillationMap.GetCurveVLLE(out m_vXCurve, out dbPlaitTemp);
				m_IDistillationMap.GetTieLinesVLLE(out m_vTieLines, out m_vVaporPoints);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void Calculate_LLEenvelope()
		{
			//IL_006e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			m_vXCurve = null;
			m_vTieLines = null;
			m_IDistillationMap.Temperature = AzeoGUIGlobals.KTemp(m_dbTemp, m_sTempUnits);
			try
			{
				m_IDistillationMap.ComputeEnvelopeLLE();
				m_IDistillationMap.GetCurveLLE(out m_vXCurve);
				m_IDistillationMap.GetTieLinesLLE(out m_vTieLines);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void SendDistValues()
		{
			//IL_0096: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				m_IDistillationMap.Pressure = AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits);
				m_IDistillationMap.SetComponentData(m_vPureCompNames[0], m_vPureCompNames[1], m_vPureCompNames[2]);
				m_IDistillationMap.SetPropertyData(m_szVLEModel, m_szVLEModel, m_szLLEModel, m_nPhase);
				m_IDistillationMap.GetPureProperties(out m_vPureComp, out m_vPureTemps, out m_vPureStability);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void GTMolarWTS()
		{
			if (m_vPureCompNames != null && m_vPureCompNames.Length >= 2)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
				{
					MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt[i] = GetMoleWt(m_vPureCompNames[i]);
				}
				m_vPureMole = MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt;
			}
		}

		public void ResetComposition()
		{
			m_CompDistArray = null;
		}

		public int ComputeFeed()
		{
			//IL_0104: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (m_szComp == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			int ncc = m_szComp.Length;
			mvarErrorStore.ClearErrors();
			if (m_CompDistArray == null || m_CompDistArray.Length == 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			try
			{
				object vaX_Feed = new VariantWrapper(m_vXFeed);
				object vBasis_Comp = new VariantWrapper(m_vBasisComp);
				m_IDistillationMap.ComputeFeed(ncc, AzeoGUIGlobals.gNumOfComponents, m_szPropModel, m_nPhase, AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits), m_vInStreamData, m_CompDistArray, (int)m_nBasis, (int)m_nEnthalpyMethod, m_vMole, out vaX_Feed, out m_dbFlow, out m_dbFeedQuality, out m_dbFeedTemp, out m_nBasisComp, out vBasis_Comp, out m_nErr, out m_nMsg);
				m_vXFeed = (double[])vaX_Feed;
				m_vBasisComp = (int[])vBasis_Comp;
				int nErr = m_nErr;
				mvarErrorStore.AddError(nErr);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(ex.Message, PlotFormsResources.Error, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			return m_nErr;
		}

		public bool CalcMaterialBalance(ref int code)
		{
			try
			{
				int[,] array = new int[3, 4];
				if (m_Entered.Length == 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				m_nMatTot = 0;
				for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
					{
						array[i, j] = ((m_Entered[i, j] != 0) ? 1 : 0);
						m_nMatTot += ((m_Entered[i, j] != 0) ? 1 : 0);
					}
				}
				if (m_nMatTot != 3)
				{
					return false;
				}
				object vaXdist = m_DistilComp;
				object vaXbot = m_BotComp;
				object vaRecovDist = m_DistRecovery;
				object vaRecovBot = m_BotRecovery;
				object vaXdistStream = m_vDistStream;
				object vaXBotStream = m_vbotStream;
				object vaXdecTieline = m_vDecanterTieLine;
				object vaYdec = m_vDecEquilibrium;
				m_IDistillationMap.ComputeMaterialBalance(AzeoGUIGlobals.gNumOfComponents, m_szPropModel, m_nPhase, AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits), 2, (int)m_Decanter, m_vXFeed, m_dbFlow, array, (int)m_nBasis, m_vPureMole, m_idx, ref vaXdist, ref vaXbot, ref vaRecovDist, ref vaRecovBot, out vaXdistStream, out vaXBotStream, out m_dbDFlow, out m_dbBFlow, out vaXdecTieline, out vaYdec, out m_dbPhiProduct, out m_dbDistTemp, out m_dbBotTemp, out m_nErr, out m_nMsg, out m_nConverg);
				m_DistilComp = (double[])vaXdist;
				m_BotComp = (double[])vaXbot;
				m_DistRecovery = (double[])vaRecovDist;
				m_BotRecovery = (double[])vaRecovBot;
				m_vDistStream = (double[])vaXdistStream;
				m_vbotStream = (double[])vaXBotStream;
				m_vDecanterTieLine = (double[,])vaXdecTieline;
				m_vDecEquilibrium = (double[])vaYdec;
				MTriGlobals.CopyCompositionForDisplay(m_DistilComp, m_BotComp);
				if (m_nBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref MTriGlobals.gvXDistDisplay, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref MTriGlobals.gvXBotDisplay, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
				}
				if (m_nErr >= 2500 && m_nErr < 2600)
				{
					code = m_nErr;
				}
				else if (m_nMode == 1 && m_nConverg == 1 && m_nErr >= 2600 && m_nErr < 2700)
				{
					code = 2508;
				}
				else if (m_nMode == 0 && m_nConverg == 1 && m_nErr >= 2600 && m_nErr < 2700)
				{
					code = 2615;
				}
				else if (m_nMode == 1 && m_nConverg == 1)
				{
					code = 2508;
				}
				else if (m_nMode == 0 && m_nConverg == 1)
				{
					code = 2615;
				}
				else
				{
					code = m_nErr;
				}
				mvarErrorStore.AddError(code);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(ex.Message);
				code = 2500;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public int Compute_Design()
		{
			try
			{
				m_vX_Reflux = new double[3];
				m_vY_top = new double[3];
				object vx_Reflux = m_vX_Reflux;
				object vy_Top = m_vY_top;
				object vStage_Col = m_vstage_col;
				object vx_Col = m_vx_col;
				object vy_Col = m_vy_col;
				object vT_Col = m_vT_col;
				object vT_est = m_vT_Est;
				object vX_est = m_vX_Est;
				object vY_est = m_vY_Est;
				Trace.WriteLine($"AzeoGUIGlobals.gNumOfComponents = {AzeoGUIGlobals.gNumOfComponents}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_szPropModel = {m_szPropModel}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_nPhase = {m_nPhase}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits) = {AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits)}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_Decanter = {m_Decanter}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_nMaxStages = {m_nMaxStages}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_nStopCriteria = {m_nStopCriteria}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_dbFlow = {m_dbFlow}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_dbFeedQuality = {m_dbFeedQuality}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_nStopCriteria = {m_DistilComp[0]}, {m_DistilComp[1]}, {m_DistilComp[2]}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_dbBFlow = {m_dbBFlow}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_vDecSpec = {m_vDecSpec[0]}, {m_vDecSpec[1]}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_dbDecanterVal = {m_dbDecanterVal}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_RefluxReboiler = {m_RefluxReboiler}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_InpRefluxRatio = {m_InpRefluxRatio}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_RefluxReboiler = {m_RefluxReboiler}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_InpReboilerRatio = {m_InpReboilerRatio}");
				Trace.WriteLine($"m_dbPhiReflux = {m_dbPhiReflux}");
				m_IDistillationMap.BV_Design(AzeoGUIGlobals.gNumOfComponents, m_szPropModel, m_nPhase, AzeoGUIGlobals.KPressure(m_dbPressure, m_sPressureUnits), (int)m_Decanter, m_nMaxStages, (int)m_nStopCriteria, m_dbFlow, m_dbFeedQuality, m_DistilComp, m_dbDFlow, m_BotComp, m_dbBFlow, m_vDecSpec, m_dbDecanterVal, (int)(m_RefluxReboiler + 1), ref m_InpRefluxRatio, ref m_InpReboilerRatio, ref m_dbPhiReflux, ref vx_Reflux, ref vy_Top, out m_nfeasible, out m_dbStages_Total, out m_dbStages_Feed, out m_nn_rect, out m_vx_rect, out m_nn_strip, out m_vx_strip, out m_nRectFeasibility, out m_vRectFeasibilty, out m_nStripFeasibility, out m_vStripFeasibility, out m_nn_col, out vStage_Col, out vx_Col, out vy_Col, out vT_Col, out m_dbV_rect, out m_dbV_strip, out m_dbl_rect, out m_dbL_strip, out m_dbq_reb, out m_dbq_con, out m_nRnstages, out m_nFeedStages, out m_dbL1_Spec, out m_dbL2_Spec, out m_nComp_List, out vT_est, out vX_est, out vY_est, out m_nErr, out m_nMsg);
				m_vX_Reflux = (double[])vx_Reflux;
				m_vY_top = (double[])vy_Top;
				m_vstage_col = (double[])vStage_Col;
				m_vstage_col = (double[])vStage_Col;
				m_vx_col = (double[,])vx_Col;
				m_vy_col = (double[,])vy_Col;
				m_vT_col = (double[])vT_Col;
				m_vT_Est = (double[])vT_est;
				m_vX_Est = vX_est as double[,];
				m_vY_Est = vY_est as double[,];
				MTriGlobals.CopyCompositionForDisplay(m_DistilComp, m_BotComp);
				if (m_nBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref MTriGlobals.gvXDistDisplay, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
					AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref MTriGlobals.gvXBotDisplay, ref MTriGlobals.Mol_Wt, BasisType.BT_Mass, 3);
				}
				int nErr = m_nErr;
				mvarErrorStore.AddError(nErr);
				return m_nErr;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				AzeoGUIGlobals.ErrorBomb(ex.Message);
				return 2500;
			}
		}

		public bool ValidateMBVal()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (m_Entered != null && m_Entered.Length != 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
					{
						if (m_Entered[j, i] != 0)
						{
							num = GetMappedValue(j, i);
							if (num > 1.0)
							{
								return false;
							}
							num2 += num;
							if (num2 > 1.0)
							{
								return false;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private double GetMappedValue(int i, int j)
		{
			i--;
			return j switch
			{
				1 => m_DistilComp[i], 
				2 => m_BotComp[i], 
				3 => m_DistRecovery[i], 
				4 => m_BotRecovery[i], 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}
