		public void SetEntered(long i, long j, long lData)
		{
			mvarm_Entered[(int)checked((nint)i), (int)checked((nint)j)] = lData;
			CheckCompleteness(asInpFlags.asiMentered, Check_m_Entered());
		}

		public bool CheckInputs()
		{
			int num = 0;
			long num2 = 0L;
			long num3 = 0L;
			DesSpecInputs = 1L;
			long num4 = 0L;
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				asInpFlags asInpFlags2;
				for (asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiMaxIterations; asInpFlags2 <= asInpFlags.asiStopCriteria; asInpFlags2++)
				{
					if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
					{
						num++;
						num4++;
					}
				}
				AdvInpFlag = num4 == 7;
				for (asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiMode; asInpFlags2 <= asInpFlags.asiDecanter; asInpFlags2++)
				{
					if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
					{
						num++;
						num2++;
					}
				}
				MainSpecFlag = num2 == MainSpecInputs;
				asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiMentered;
				if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
				{
					num++;
					num3++;
				}
				if (mvarm_nMode == asMode.asDesignMode)
				{
					asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiRefluxReboiler;
					DesSpecInputs = 3L;
					if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
					{
						num++;
						num3++;
					}
					asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiRRRatio;
					if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
					{
						num++;
						num3++;
					}
					if (mvarm_m_Decanter == asDecConfiguration.asDecanter)
					{
						asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiOptDecVapRef;
						DesSpecInputs = 6L;
						if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
						{
							num++;
							num3++;
						}
						if (mvarOptDecVapRef == asDecVapRef.asiSinglePhase)
						{
							DesSpecInputs = 5L;
							asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiDecComponent;
							if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
							{
								num++;
								num3++;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiDecComponent;
							if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
							{
								num++;
								num3++;
							}
							asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiDecComposition;
							if (InputFlags[(int)asInpFlags2])
							{
								num++;
								num3++;
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiRRRatio;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					asInpFlags2 = asInpFlags.asiMentered;
				}
				if (mvarLLEnvelope)
				{
					asInpFlags2++;
					if (InputFlags[25])
					{
						num++;
						TInputFlag = true;
					}
					else
					{
						TInputFlag = false;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					TInputFlag = true;
				}
				if (num == (int)asInpFlags2)
				{
					result = true;
					IsComplete = true;
				}
				if (num3 == DesSpecInputs)
				{
					DesSpecFlag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					DesSpecFlag = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string ListInputs()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			for (int i = 0; i < 29; i++)
			{
				text = text + i + " : " + InputFlags[i] + "\n";
			}
			text = text + "\nM_entered count = " + m_entcount;
			text = text + "\nDesSpecFlag = " + DesSpecFlag;
			return text + "\nDesSpecInputs = " + DesSpecInputs;
		}

		public bool DoCheck()
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				if (InputChecking)
				{
					bool num = CheckInputs();
					if (FireEvents)
					{
						if (this.OnMainSpecComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnMainSpecComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(MainSpecFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnDesSpecComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnDesSpecComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(DesSpecFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnAdvInpComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnAdvInpComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(AdvInpFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnTernaryInputComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnTernaryInputComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(TInputFlag));
						}
					}
					if (num && FireEvents)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void RaiseInputChangedEvent()
		{
			if (this.OnInputChanged != null)
			{
				this.OnInputChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void RaiseInputCompleteEvent()
		{
			if (this.OnInputComplete != null)
			{
				this.OnInputComplete(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void RaiseInputEvents()
		{
			if (FireEvents)
			{
				RaiseInputChangedEvent();
				if (DoCheck())
				{
					RaiseInputCompleteEvent();
				}
			}
		}

		private bool CheckCompleteness(asInpFlags InpFlag, bool bValue)
		{
			bool result = true;
			InputFlags[(int)InpFlag] = bValue;
			CalcOK = false;
			try
			{
				if (FireEvents && this.OnInputChanged != null)
				{
					this.OnInputChanged(this, new EventArgs());
				}
				if (InputChecking)
				{
					bool num = CheckInputs();
					if (FireEvents)
					{
						if (this.OnMainSpecComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnMainSpecComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(MainSpecFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnDesSpecComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnDesSpecComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(DesSpecFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnAdvInpComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnAdvInpComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(AdvInpFlag));
						}
						if (this.OnTernaryInputComplete != null)
						{
							this.OnTernaryInputComplete(this, new InputCompletedEventArgs(TInputFlag));
						}
					}
					if (num && FireEvents && this.OnInputComplete != null)
					{
						this.OnInputComplete(this, new EventArgs());
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool Check_m_Entered()
		{
			bool result = false;
			m_entcount = 0L;
			for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= 3; j++)
				{
					if (mvarm_Entered[i, j] == 1)
					{
						m_entcount++;
					}
				}
			}
			if (m_entcount == 3)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class InputCompletedEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public bool CompletedValue { get; set; }

		public InputCompletedEventArgs(bool completed)
		{
			CompletedValue = completed;
		}
	}
	public class CurveObj : IPlotobj

		public void RemoveObj()
		{
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				if (AzeoGUIGlobals.g_BugLog)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.TraceToFile("IPlotobj_RemoveObj - CurveObj", AzeoGUIGlobals.g_LogFile, NewFile: false);
				}
				_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, (short)(-1), (short)0);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.Error + ex.Message + PlotFormsResources.InCrvRemove, MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void AddObj()
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			double[] array = new double[3];
			if (_Type == ObjType.MarkAzeotrope)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.ErrorTypeOfCrv, PlotFormsResources.BadProAddObjError, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			if (_ID == -1)
			{
				_ID = _MyPlot.NewLine(_Prop[1], _Prop[0], _Prop[2], _Prop[3], _UserData);
				if (_Type == ObjType.CurveDistillBound || _Type == ObjType.MatBalLine || _Type == ObjType.TieLineDecanter || _Type == ObjType.Rectify || _Type == ObjType.FeasibleRetify || _Type == ObjType.Strip || _Type == ObjType.FeasibleStrip)
				{
					_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, (short)1, (short)3);
				}
			}
			else if (_Type == ObjType.Strip && !AzeoGUIGlobals.gStripMarkerState)
			{
				_MyPlot.SetLineAttribute(_ID, _Prop[1], _Prop[0], (short)0, _Prop[3], (short)0, (short)15);
			}
			else if (_Type == ObjType.Rectify && !AzeoGUIGlobals.gRectifyMarkerState)
			{
				_MyPlot.SetLineAttribute(_ID, _Prop[1], _Prop[0], (short)0, _Prop[3], (short)0, (short)15);
			}
			else
			{
				_MyPlot.SetLineAttribute(_ID, _Prop[1], _Prop[0], _Prop[2], _Prop[3], (short)0, (short)15);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i <= _Points.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				if (_Points[i, MTriGlobals.CO1] >= -1E-06 && _Points[i, MTriGlobals.CO2] <= 1.00001)
				{
					array[0] = _Points[i, 0];
					array[1] = _Points[i, 1];
					array[2] = _Points[i, 2];
					MTriGlobals.Transformers(_Points[i, MTriGlobals.CO1], _Points[i, MTriGlobals.CO2], ref array[0], ref array[1], ref array[2]);
					_MyPlot.SetXYData(_ID, (short)i, array[MTriGlobals.CO1], array[MTriGlobals.CO2]);
				}
			}
			if (!_Visible)
			{
				DisplayObj(state: false);
			}
		}

		public void DisplayObj(bool state)
		{
			_Visible = state;
			short num = (short)(state ? 1 : 0);
			_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, num, (short)1);
		}

		public void SetdbPointsArr(int index1, int index2, double dbArr)
		{
			_Points[index1, index2] = dbArr;
		}

		public double GetdbPointsArr(int index1, int index2)
		{
			return _Points[index1, index2];
		}

		public void SetPProperties(PropertyType attr, object o)
		{
			_Prop[(int)attr] = (short)(int)o;
		}
	}

	public class ErrorEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public int Flag { get; private set; }

		public int Code { get; private set; }

		public ErrorEventArgs(int flag, int code)
		{
			Flag = flag;
			Code = code;
		}
	}
	public class ErrorCodes
	{
		public delegate void ErrorEventHandler(object source, ErrorEventArgs e);

		private List<int> m_errCodes;

		public event ErrorEventHandler OnError;

		public ErrorCodes()
		{
			m_errCodes = new List<int>();
		}

		~ErrorCodes()
		{
			m_errCodes = null;
		}

		public void ClearErrors()
		{
			if (m_errCodes.Count > 0)
			{
				m_errCodes.Clear();
			}
		}

		public int CountErrors()
		{
			return m_errCodes.Count;
		}

		public void AddError(int Code)
		{
			try
			{
				if (Code >= 2500 && Code < 2600)
				{
					m_errCodes.Add(Code);
					ErrorEventArgs e = new ErrorEventArgs(2, Code);
					if (this.OnError != null)
					{
						this.OnError(this, e);
					}
				}
				else if (Code >= 2600 && Code < 2700)
				{
					m_errCodes.Add(Code);
					ErrorEventArgs e2 = new ErrorEventArgs(1, Code);
					if (this.OnError != null)
					{
						this.OnError(this, e2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ErrorEventArgs e3 = new ErrorEventArgs(0, Code);
					if (this.OnError != null)
					{
						this.OnError(this, e3);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

			string text = ((_Type != ObjType.TextTemperature) ? _UserData.ToString() : (AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(result, _TempUnit).ToString("0.0#") + " " + _TempUnit));
			MTriGlobals.Transformers(_Points[0, MTriGlobals.CO1], _Points[0, MTriGlobals.CO2], ref array[0], ref array[1], ref array[2]);
			if (_ID == -1)

				_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, (short)8, (short)8);

			else
			{

			}
			_MyPlot.SetTextAttribute(_ID, (short)0, (short)1, (short)0, _Prop[6].ToString(), (short)(int)_Prop[7], (short)0);
			if (!_Visible)
			{
				DisplayObj(state: false);
			}
		}

				m_aDelcase = value;
			}
		}

		public Consep m_CConsep
		{
			get
			{
				return mvarm_CConsep;
			}
			set
			{
				mvarm_CConsep = value;
			}
		}

		public int CalcObjects()
		{
			int result = 0;
			for (chkView chkView2 = chkView.CV_Azeotropes; chkView2 <= chkView.CV_Decanter; chkView2++)
			{
				m_aUsrSelected[(int)chkView2] = m_aUsrSelectCalc[(int)chkView2];
			}
			m_CConsep.SendDistValues();
			m_CConsep.GTMolarWTS();
			if (m_aUsrSelectCalc[0] && m_aRecalc[0])
			{
				m_CConsep.Calculate_Azeotropes();
			}
			if (m_aUsrSelectCalc[1] && m_aRecalc[1])
			{
				m_CConsep.Calculate_DistBoundry();
			}
			if (m_aUsrSelectCalc[10] && m_aRecalc[10])
			{
				m_CConsep.Calculate_VLLEenvelope();
			}
			else if (m_aUsrSelectCalc[9] && m_aRecalc[9])
			{
				m_CConsep.Calculate_LLEenvelope();
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			num = m_CConsep.ComputeFeed();
			if (num >= 2500 && num < 2700)
			{
				num2 = HandleErrors(num);
				if (num2 > 0)
				{
					return num2;
				}
				switch (num2)
				{
				case -1:
					m_CConsep.m_nEnthalpyMethod = asEnthalpyMethod.asiFeedEnthalpy;
					break;
				case -2:
					m_CConsep.m_dbFeedQuality = 1.0;
					break;
				}
			}
			bool flag = true;
			if (m_CConsep.m_nMatTot == 3)
			{
				flag = m_CConsep.CalcMaterialBalance(ref num);
				if (num >= 2500 && num < 2700)
				{
					num2 = HandleErrors(num);
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						return num2;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (m_CConsep.m_nMode == 1 && flag)
			{
				num = m_CConsep.Compute_Design();
				if (num >= 2500 && num < 2700)
				{
					num2 = HandleErrors(num);
					if (num2 > 0)
					{
						return num2;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private int HandleErrors(int Error)
		{
			int result = 0;
			switch (Error)
			{
			case 2500:
				result = 1;
				break;
			case 2501:
				result = 2;
				break;
			case 2502:
			case 2503:
			case 2504:
			case 2505:
			case 2506:
			case 2507:
				result = 3;
				break;
			case 2508:
				result = 4;
				break;
			case 2600:
				result = -1;
				break;
			case 2601:
				result = -2;
				break;
			case 2602:
			case 2603:
			case 2604:
			case 2605:
			case 2606:
			case 2607:
			case 2608:
			case 2609:
			case 2610:
			case 2611:
			case 2612:
			case 2613:
			case 2614:
				result = -3;
				break;
			case 2615:
				result = 4;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}

		public int iNumberOfPoints

			}

		public void RemoveObj()
		{
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				if (AzeoGUIGlobals.g_BugLog)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.TraceToFile("IPlotobj_RemoveObj - CurveObj", AzeoGUIGlobals.g_LogFile, NewFile: false);
				}
				_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, (short)(-1), (short)0);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.Error + ex.Message + PlotFormsResources.InCrvRemove, MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void AddObj()
		{
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			double[] array = new double[3];
			if (_Type == ObjType.NoSpecificType)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.ErrorTypeOfCrv, PlotFormsResources.BadProAddObjError, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)48, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			if (_ID == -1)
			{
				_ID = _MyPlot.NewLine(_Prop[1], (short)(-1), _Prop[2], _Prop[3], (short)0);
				if (_Type == ObjType.MarkMBProd || _Type == ObjType.MarkFeed || _Type == ObjType.TextFeed || _Type == ObjType.MarkAzeotrope || _Type == ObjType.RectifyMarker || _Type == ObjType.StripMarker || _Type == ObjType.YTopMarker || _Type == ObjType.YTopLabel)
				{
					_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, (short)1, (short)3);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_MyPlot.SetLineAttribute(_ID, _Prop[1], (short)(-1), _Prop[2], _Prop[3], (short)0, (short)13);
			}
			array[0] = _Points[0, 0];
			array[1] = _Points[0, 1];
			array[2] = _Points[0, 2];
			MTriGlobals.Transformers(_Points[0, MTriGlobals.CO1], _Points[0, MTriGlobals.CO2], ref array[0], ref array[1], ref array[2]);
			_MyPlot.SetXYData(_ID, (short)0, array[MTriGlobals.CO1], array[MTriGlobals.CO2]);
			if (!_Visible)
			{
				DisplayObj(state: false);
			}
		}

		public void DisplayObj(bool state)
		{
			_Visible = state;
			short num = (short)(state ? 1 : 0);
			_MyPlot.SetObject(_ID, num, (short)1);
		}

		public void SetdbPointsArr(int index1, int index2, double dbArr)
		{
			_Points[index1, index2] = dbArr;
		}

		public double GetdbPointsArr(int index1, int index2)
		{
			return _Points[index1, index2];
		}

		public void SetPProperties(PropertyType attr, object o)
		{
			_Prop[(int)attr] = (short)(int)o;
		}
	}

	public class MTriGlobals
	{
		public static readonly int TOTALPROPERTIES = 8;

		public static Point LineStart = new Point();

		public static int CalcBoilPoint;

		public static int CoDisplay;

		public static long ToolBarPos;

		public static CoordPair CoordMode;

		public static int CO1;

		public static int CO2;

		public static double[] Mol_Wt = new double[3];

		public static BasisType TPBasis;

		public static int currentbasis;

		public static double[] gvXDistDisplay = new double[3];

		public static double[] gvXBotDisplay = new double[3];

		public static double[] gvXDistRecDisplay = new double[3];

		public static double[] gvXBotRecDisplay = new double[3];

		public static void ProcessCoordinates(double c1, double c2, ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			double num = 1.0 - c1 - c2;
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.xy:
				X = c1;
				Y = c2;
				Z = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				X = c1;
				Y = num;
				Z = c2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				X = c2;
				Y = c1;
				Z = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				X = num;
				Y = c1;
				Z = c2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				X = c2;
				Y = num;
				Z = c1;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				X = num;
				Y = c2;
				Z = c1;
				break;
			}
		}

		public static void Transformers(double c1, double c2, ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			double[] Composition_Array = new double[3];
			ProcessCoordinates(c1, c2, ref X, ref Y, ref Z);
			Composition_Array[0] = X;
			Composition_Array[1] = Y;
			Composition_Array[2] = Z;
			AzeoGUIGlobals.ConvertBasis(Way.W_In, ref Composition_Array, ref Mol_Wt, TPBasis, 3);
			X = Composition_Array[0];
			Y = Composition_Array[1];
			Z = Composition_Array[2];
		}

		public static void SortCoords(ref double X, ref double Y, ref double Z)
		{
			switch (CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.XZ:
			{
				double num = Y;
				Y = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.YX:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Y;
				Y = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.YZ:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Z;
				Z = Y;
				Y = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.ZX:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Y;
				Y = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.ZY:
			{
				double num = X;
				X = Z;
				Z = num;
				break;
			}
			case CoordPair.xy:
				break;
			}
		}

		public static void SetMoleWt(double v1, double v2, double v3)
		{
			Mol_Wt[0] = v1;
			Mol_Wt[1] = v2;
			Mol_Wt[2] = v3;
		}

		public static void CopyCompositionForDisplay(double[] DistilComp, double[] botcomp)
		{
			if (DistilComp != null && DistilComp.Length >= 3)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
				{
					gvXDistDisplay[i] = DistilComp[i];
				}
			}
			if (botcomp != null && botcomp.Length >= 3)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= 2; j++)
				{
					gvXBotDisplay[j] = botcomp[j];
				}
			}
		}

		public static void CopyRecoveryForDisplay(double[] DistilRecovery, double[] botRecovery)
		{
			if (DistilRecovery.Length != 3 || botRecovery.Length != 3)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i <= 2; i++)
			{
				gvXDistRecDisplay[i] = DistilRecovery[i];
				gvXBotRecDisplay[i] = botRecovery[i];
			}
		}
	}

			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaRelaseRfsException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);

		public void InitPlot()
		{
			new ReadWriteEasyReg();
			Properties_Default();
			Properties_Load();
			oMyPlot.SetObject((short)3, (short)1, (short)3);
			oMyPlot.SetObject((short)2, (short)1, (short)3);
			oMyPlot.SetEvent((short)1, true, true);
			oMyPlot.SetEvent((short)2, true, true);
			oMyPlot.SetEvent((short)3, true, false);
			oMyPlot.SetEvent((short)4, true, false);
			oMyPlot.SetEvent((short)7, true, false);
			oMyPlot.Title = "";
			MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.xy;
			SetCoordinates();
			PlotType = PlotType.RightAngled;
			oMyPlot.DisplayType = 2;
			oMyPlot.SetCopyMode((short)1);
			oMyPlot.SetHighlightMode((short)1);
			oMyPlot.GridType = 0;
		}

		public void InitObj(IPlotobj MyObj, ObjType oType)
		{
			MyObj.eObjType = oType;
			MyObj.PlotControl = oMyPlot;
			MyObj.sDisplayTempUnit = DisplayTempUnit;
			switch (MyObj.eObjType)
			{
			case ObjType.TextTemperature:
			case ObjType.TextUser:
			case ObjType.TextMBProd:
			case ObjType.TextFeed:
			case ObjType.YTopLabel:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PFontName, Properties.PFont.PName);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PFontSize, Properties.PFont.PSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkAzeotrope:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.CurveDistillBound:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDistBoundry.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.EnvLLE:
			case ObjType.EnvVLLE:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PEnvelope.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PPattern, Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern);
				break;
			case ObjType.TieLineLLE:
			case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
			case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
			case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PTieLine.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker2, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.CurveResidue:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.CurveDistill:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkUserPoint:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PUserMarker.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.FreeHandLine:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PUserLine.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PUserLine.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PUserLine.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MatBalLine:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PMBalance.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PMBalance.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PMBalance.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PMBalance.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkMBProd:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PProdMarker.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PProdMarker.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PProdMarker.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkFeed:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PfeedMarker.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.Strip:
			case ObjType.FeasibleStrip:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PStrip.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PStrip.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PStrip.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PStrip.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.Rectify:
			case ObjType.FeasibleRetify:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PRect.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PRect.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PRect.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PRect.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.TieLineDecanter:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDecanterTie.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDecanterTie.PLine);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.YTopMarker:
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PYTop.PColour);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PYTop.PMarker);
				MyObj.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PYTop.PMarkerSize);
				break;
			case ObjType.MarkVLLEUser:
			case ObjType.CurveUser:
			case ObjType.MarkDecanter:
			case ObjType.RectifyMarker:
			case ObjType.StripMarker:
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ClearObjs(ObjType SpecificObjType)
		{
			foreach (IPlotobj item in cPObj.Where(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> obj)
			{
				KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> keyValuePair = obj;
				return keyValuePair.Value.eObjType == SpecificObjType || SpecificObjType == ObjType.NoSpecificType;
			}).Select(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> obj)
			{
				KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> keyValuePair = obj;
				return keyValuePair.Value;
			}).ToList())
			{
				CollectionRemoveObj(item);
			}
		}

		public void SetCoordinates()
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			short num = 0;
			short num2 = 1;
			short num3 = 2;
			switch (MTriGlobals.CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.CPNone:
				MMMessageBox.Show(PlotFormsResources.ErrorSetInitCoordPair, PlotFormsResources.BadProError, (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)16, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				break;
			case CoordPair.xy:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num2;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num3;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num2;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num2;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num3;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num3;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				MTriGlobals.CO1 = num3;
				MTriGlobals.CO2 = num2;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void RotateObjs()
		{
			switch (MTriGlobals.CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.xy:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.YZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.ZX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.xy;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.XZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.ZY;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.YX;
				break;
			}
			string text = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-3)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-3)) - 1] = text;
			double num = PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-3)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-3)) - 1] = num;
			SetCoordinates();
			Redraw();
		}

		public void FlipObjs()
		{
			switch (MTriGlobals.CoordMode)
			{
			case CoordPair.xy:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.ZY;
				break;
			case CoordPair.XZ:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.YZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YX:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.ZX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.YZ:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.XZ;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZX:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.YX;
				break;
			case CoordPair.ZY:
				MTriGlobals.CoordMode = CoordPair.xy;
				break;
			}
			string text = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1] = text;
			double num = PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-1)) - 1] = PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1];
			PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)(-2)) - 1] = num;
			SetCoordinates();
			Redraw();
		}

		public void DisplayObj(bool bState, ObjType WhatObj)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> item in cPObj)
			{
				if (item.Value.eObjType == WhatObj)
				{
					item.Value.DisplayObj(bState);
					if (WhatObj == ObjType.TextTemperature)
					{
						RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Left, bState);
						RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Right, bState);
						RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Top, bState);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void MousePointer(int shape)
		{
			oMyPlot.SetMousePointer((short)shape);
		}

		public IPlotobj FindObjByID(int myid)
		{
			try
			{
				string key = "po" + myid;
				return cPObj[key];
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return null;
		}

		public IPlotobj FindObjByType(ObjType ObjTyp)
		{
			return cPObj.Where(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> obj)
			{
				KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> keyValuePair = obj;
				return keyValuePair.Value.eObjType == ObjTyp;
			}).Select(delegate(KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> obj)
			{
				KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> keyValuePair = obj;
				return keyValuePair.Value;
			}).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public void PrintSetup()
		{
			oMyPlot.Print2(true);
		}

		public void PrintPreview(bool Stat)
		{
			oMyPlot.PrintPreview(Stat);
		}

		public void PrintArea()
		{
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				oMyPlot.PageLayout();
				oMyPlot.Print2(false);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaPrintAreaException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			AddRightAngleLabels();
			oMyPlot.UpdateAxis();
		}

		public void CollectionAddObj(IPlotobj obj)
		{
			//IL_0056: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				string key = "po" + obj.iObjID;
				if (obj.iObjID != -1)
				{
					cPObj.Add(key, obj);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaAddCollectionObjException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		public void CollectionRemoveObj(IPlotobj obj)
		{
			//IL_0053: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				string key = "po" + obj.iObjID;
				obj.RemoveObj();
				cPObj.Remove(key);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaRemoveCollectionObjException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
				if (AzeoGUIGlobals.g_BugLog)
				{
					AzeoGUIGlobals.TraceToFile("CollectionRemoveObj - Error " + ex.Message, AzeoGUIGlobals.g_LogFile, NewFile: false);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ShowBalloon(string Message)
		{
			oMyPlot.DisplayText(Message, (short)LastMX, (short)(LastMY + 20.0), 30000);
		}

		private void RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis iaxis, bool? showTemp = null)
		{
			if (showTemp.HasValue)
			{
				_bShowTemperatureText = showTemp.Value;
			}
			string text = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs((short)iaxis) - 1];
			if (_bShowTemperatureText)
			{
				text = text + "\\n(" + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs((short)iaxis) - 1], DisplayTempUnit).ToString("#0.000") + " " + DisplayTempUnit + ")";
			}
			oMyPlot.SetLabel((short)iaxis, text);
		}

		private void AddRightAngleLabels()
		{
			RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Top);
			RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Right);
			RefreshPlotLabel(PAxis.A_Left);
			if (PlotType == PlotType.Equilateral)
			{
				oMyPlot.xlabel = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-2) - 1];
				oMyPlot.ylabel = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-3) - 1];
				oMyPlot.SetLabel((short)2, PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-1) - 1]);
				return;
			}
			oMyPlot.xlabel = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-2) - 1] + " (" + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs(-2) - 1], DisplayTempUnit).ToString("#0.000") + DisplayTempUnit + ")";
			oMyPlot.ylabel = PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-3) - 1] + " (" + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs(-3) - 1], DisplayTempUnit).ToString("#0.000") + DisplayTempUnit + ")";
			oMyPlot.SetLabel((short)2, PlotLabels.sLabel[Math.Abs(-1) - 1] + " (" + AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(PlotLabels.sPureTemp[Math.Abs(-1) - 1], DisplayTempUnit).ToString("#0.000") + DisplayTempUnit + ")");
		}

		public void Redraw()
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> item in cPObj)
			{
				if (item.Value.iObjID == -1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (item.Value.eObjType == ObjType.MarkUserPoint && item.Value.vUserData != null && int.TryParse(item.Value.vUserData.ToString(), out var result))
				{
					IPlotobj plotobj = FindObjByID(result);
					if (plotobj.eObjType == ObjType.TextUser && plotobj.vUserData != null)
					{
						string text = plotobj.vUserData.ToString();
						string unit = text.Substring(text.Length - 1);
						if (double.TryParse(text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1), out var result2))
						{
							plotobj.vUserData = AzeoGUIGlobals.CTemp(AzeoGUIGlobals.KTemp(result2, unit), DisplayTempUnit).ToString("#0.0000" + DisplayTempUnit);
						}
					}
				}
				item.Value.sDisplayTempUnit = DisplayTempUnit;
				item.Value.AddObj();
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		public void Copy()
		{
			string text = ((TPBasis == BasisType.BT_Mass) ? "Mass" : "Mole");
			oMyPlot.SetFont((short)0, "", (short)0, (short)0);
			oMyPlot.Title = "Ternary Map (" + text + " Basis)";
			oMyPlot.Copy();
			oMyPlot.SetFont((short)0, "", (short)1, (short)0);
			oMyPlot.Title = "";
		}

		public void PropertyManager(PageEnum index)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			PropertyManager propertyManager = new PropertyManager(index);
			Properties_Set(propertyManager.PropertyPage.PropPage);
			propertyManager.ShowDialog();
			Settings adjustSettings = propertyManager.PropertyPage.AdjustSettings;
			if ((int)adjustSettings != 0)
			{
				if ((int)adjustSettings == 2)
				{
					Properties_Default();
					Properties_ApplyUpdate();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Properties_Update(propertyManager.PropertyPage.PropPage);
			}
			propertyManager.Close();
		}

		private void Properties_Set(PropPage propPack)
		{
			//IL_06e7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			try
			{
				Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
				AssemblyProductAttribute assemblyProductAttribute = (AssemblyProductAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(assembly, typeof(AssemblyProductAttribute));
				AssemblyFileVersionAttribute assemblyFileVersionAttribute = (AssemblyFileVersionAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(assembly, typeof(AssemblyFileVersionAttribute));
				propPack.AboutProps.ProductName = assemblyProductAttribute.Product;
				propPack.AboutProps.Release = assembly.GetName().Version.ToString();
				propPack.AboutProps.Version = assemblyFileVersionAttribute.Version;
				propPack.GeneralProps.Coord = CoDisplay == 1;
				propPack.GeneralProps.BoilingPoint = CalcBoilPoint == 1;
				propPack.GeneralProps.Transparent = TransparentLabels == 1;
				propPack.GeneralProps.Stages = StageDisplay == 1;
				propPack.GeneralProps.ToolBarPos = ((ToolBarPosHorizontal == System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right) ? 0 : propPack.GeneralProps.ToolBarPos);
				propPack.GeneralProps.Font = Properties.PFont.PName;
				propPack.GeneralProps.FontSize = Properties.PFont.PSize;
				propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour;
				propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker;
				propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour;
				propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker;
				propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.EnvColour = (long)Properties.PEnvelope.PColour;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.EnvPatern = Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineColour = (long)Properties.PTieLine.PColour;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineStyle = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PLine;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineMarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.VapMarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2;
				propPack.EnvelopeProps.VapMarkerSize = Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve1Colour = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve1Style = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve1MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve1MarkerSize = Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve2Colour = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve2Style = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve2MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker;
				propPack.CurveProps.Curve2MarkerSize = Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PUserMarker.PColour;
				propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker;
				propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.FreeHandProps.LineColour = (long)Properties.PUserLine.PColour;
				propPack.FreeHandProps.LineStyle = (int)Properties.PUserLine.PLine;
				propPack.FreeHandProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PUserLine.PMarker;
				propPack.FreeHandProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.MatBalProps.LineColour = (long)Properties.PMBalance.PColour;
				propPack.MatBalProps.LineStyle = (int)Properties.PMBalance.PLine;
				propPack.MatBalProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PMBalance.PMarker;
				propPack.MatBalProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PMBalance.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.ProductProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PProdMarker.PColour;
				propPack.ProductProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PProdMarker.PMarker;
				propPack.ProductProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PProdMarker.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.FeedProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PfeedMarker.PColour;
				propPack.FeedProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarker;
				propPack.FeedProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1Colour = (int)Properties.PRect.PColour;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1Style = (int)Properties.PRect.PLine;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PRect.PMarker;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1MarkerSize = Properties.PRect.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2Colour = (int)Properties.PStrip.PColour;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2Style = (int)Properties.PStrip.PLine;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PStrip.PMarker;
				propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2MarkerSize = Properties.PStrip.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.DecanterProps.LineColour = (long)Properties.PDecanterTie.PColour;
				propPack.DecanterProps.LineStyle = (int)Properties.PDecanterTie.PLine;
				propPack.DecanterProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarker;
				propPack.DecanterProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarkerSize;
				propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerColour = (long)Properties.PYTop.PColour;
				propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerStyle = (int)Properties.PYTop.PMarker;
				propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerSize = Properties.PYTop.PMarkerSize;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaPropertiesSetException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
		}

		private void Properties_Update(PropPage propPack)
		{
			CoDisplay = (propPack.GeneralProps.Coord ? 1 : 0);
			CalcBoilPoint = (propPack.GeneralProps.BoilingPoint ? 1 : 0);
			if (propPack.GeneralProps.Transparent)
			{
				TransparentLabels = 1;
				oMyPlot.SetTextMode((short)1);
			}
			else
			{
				TransparentLabels = 0;
				oMyPlot.SetTextMode((short)0);
			}
			StageDisplay = (propPack.GeneralProps.Stages ? 1 : 0);
			long toolBarPos = propPack.GeneralProps.ToolBarPos;
			if ((ulong)toolBarPos <= 4uL)
			{
				switch (toolBarPos)
				{
				case 0L:
					ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					ToolBarPosVertical = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					break;
				case 1L:
					ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					ToolBarPosVertical = VerticalAlignment.Top;
					break;
				case 2L:
					ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					ToolBarPosVertical = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
					break;
				case 3L:
					ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
					ToolBarPosVertical = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					break;
				case 4L:
					ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
					ToolBarPosVertical = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (ToolBarPosHorizontal == System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left)
			{
				ToolBarPosHorizontal = System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right;
			}
			Properties.PFont.PName = propPack.GeneralProps.Font;
			Properties.PFont.PSize = propPack.GeneralProps.FontSize;
			Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize = propPack.AzeoProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize = propPack.BoundayProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PEnvelope.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.EnvelopeProps.EnvColour;
			Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern = propPack.EnvelopeProps.EnvPatern;
			Properties.PTieLine.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineColour;
			Properties.PTieLine.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineStyle;
			Properties.PTieLine.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.EnvelopeProps.LineMarkerStyle;
			Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2 = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.EnvelopeProps.VapMarkerStyle;
			Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize = propPack.EnvelopeProps.VapMarkerSize;
			Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.CurveProps.Curve1Colour;
			Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.CurveProps.Curve1Style;
			Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.CurveProps.Curve1MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize = propPack.CurveProps.Curve1MarkerSize;
			Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.CurveProps.Curve2Colour;
			Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.CurveProps.Curve2Style;
			Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.CurveProps.Curve2MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize = propPack.CurveProps.Curve2MarkerSize;
			Properties.PUserMarker.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize = propPack.MarkerProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PUserLine.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.FreeHandProps.LineColour;
			Properties.PUserLine.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.FreeHandProps.LineStyle;
			Properties.PUserLine.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.FreeHandProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize = propPack.FreeHandProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PMBalance.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.MatBalProps.LineColour;
			Properties.PMBalance.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.MatBalProps.LineStyle;
			Properties.PMBalance.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.MatBalProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PMBalance.PMarkerSize = propPack.MatBalProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PProdMarker.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.ProductProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PProdMarker.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.ProductProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PProdMarker.PMarkerSize = propPack.ProductProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PfeedMarker.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.FeedProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.FeedProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarkerSize = propPack.FeedProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PRect.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1Colour;
			Properties.PRect.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1Style;
			Properties.PRect.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PRect.PMarkerSize = propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve1MarkerSize;
			Properties.PStrip.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2Colour;
			Properties.PStrip.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2Style;
			Properties.PStrip.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PStrip.PMarkerSize = propPack.ProfilesProps.Curve2MarkerSize;
			Properties.PDecanterTie.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.DecanterProps.LineColour;
			Properties.PDecanterTie.PLine = (TPLine)propPack.DecanterProps.LineStyle;
			Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.DecanterProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarkerSize = propPack.DecanterProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties.PYTop.PColour = (TPColour)propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerColour;
			Properties.PYTop.PMarker = (TPMarkerShape)propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerStyle;
			Properties.PYTop.PMarkerSize = propPack.ColumnVapourProps.MarkerSize;
			Properties_ApplyUpdate();
		}

		private void Properties_ApplyUpdate()
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> item in cPObj)
			{
				switch (item.Value.eObjType)
				{
				case ObjType.TextTemperature:
				case ObjType.TextUser:
				case ObjType.TextMBProd:
				case ObjType.TextFeed:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PFontName, Properties.PFont.PName);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PFontSize, Properties.PFont.PSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkAzeotrope:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveDistillBound:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDistBoundry.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.EnvLLE:
				case ObjType.EnvVLLE:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PEnvelope.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PPattern, Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern);
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineVLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserVLLE:
				case ObjType.TieLineUserLLE:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PTieLine.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker2, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkVLLE:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveResidue:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.CurveDistill:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkUserPoint:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PUserMarker.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.FreeHandLine:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PUserLine.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PUserLine.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PUserLine.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MatBalLine:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PMBalance.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PMBalance.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PMBalance.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PMBalance.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkMBProd:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PProdMarker.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PProdMarker.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PProdMarker.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.MarkFeed:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PfeedMarker.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.Strip:
				case ObjType.FeasibleStrip:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PStrip.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PStrip.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PStrip.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PStrip.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.Rectify:
				case ObjType.FeasibleRetify:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PRect.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PRect.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PRect.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PRect.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.TieLineDecanter:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PDecanterTie.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PLine, Properties.PDecanterTie.PLine);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				case ObjType.YTopMarker:
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PColour, Properties.PYTop.PColour);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarker, Properties.PYTop.PMarker);
					item.Value.SetPProperties(PropertyType.PMarkerSize, Properties.PYTop.PMarkerSize);
					break;
				}
			}
			Redraw();
		}

		private static int GetIntValueFromObj(object obj)
		{
			int.TryParse(obj.ToString(), out var result);
			return result;
		}

		private void GetEnumFromObj<TEnum>(out TEnum value, object obj) where TEnum : struct
		{
			Enum.TryParse<TEnum>(obj.ToString(), out value);
		}

		private void Properties_Load()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(REGKEY1 + "\\" + REGKEY2A + ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING + REGKEY2B);
			if (registryKey != null)
			{
				try
				{
					CoDisplay = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("COORDINATES"));
					CalcBoilPoint = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("BOILINGPOINT"));
					TransparentLabels = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("TRANSPARENTLABELS"));
					StageDisplay = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("STAGEDISPLAY"));
					Enum.TryParse<System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment>(registryKey.GetValue("TOOLBARPOSHorizontal").ToString(), out ToolBarPosHorizontal);
					Enum.TryParse<VerticalAlignment>(registryKey.GetValue("TOOLBARPOSVertical").ToString(), out ToolBarPosVertical);
					Properties.PFont.PName = registryKey.GetValue("FONTNAME").ToString();
					Properties.PFont.PSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("FONTSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PAzeotrope.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarker);
					Properties.PAzeotrope.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("AZEOTROPEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYCOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PDistBoundry.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarker);
					Properties.PDistBoundry.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("BOUNDRYMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("ENVELOPECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PEnvelope.PColour);
					Properties.PEnvelope.PPattern = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("ENVELOPEPATTERN"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINELINE").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PLine);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKER").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PMarker);
					Enum.TryParse<TPMarkerShape>(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKER2").ToString(), out Properties.PTieLine.PMarker2);
					Properties.PTieLine.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("TIELINEMARKSIZE"));
					Enum.TryParse<TPColour>(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUECOLOUR").ToString(), out Properties.PResidueCurve.PColour);
					Enum.TryParse<TPLine>(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUELINE").ToString(), out Properties.PResidueCurve.PLine);
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUEMARKER"));
					Properties.PResidueCurve.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("RESIDUEMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLLINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLMARKER"));
					Properties.PDistillationCurve.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("DISTILLMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PUserMarker.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PUserMarker.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKMARKER"));
					Properties.PUserMarker.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("USERMARKMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PUserLine.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("USERLINECOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PUserLine.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("USERLINELINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PUserLine.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("USERLINEMARKER"));
					Properties.PUserLine.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("USERLINEMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PMBalance.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("MBCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PMBalance.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("MBLINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PMBalance.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("MBMARKER"));
					Properties.PMBalance.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("MBMARKERSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PProdMarker.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("PRODCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PProdMarker.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("PRODMARKER"));
					Properties.PProdMarker.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("PRODMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PfeedMarker.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("FEEDCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("FEEDMARKER"));
					Properties.PfeedMarker.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("FEEDMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PStrip.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("STRIPCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PStrip.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("STRIPLINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PStrip.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("STRIPMARKER"));
					Properties.PStrip.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("STRIPMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PRect.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("RECTIFYCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PRect.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("RECTIFYLINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PRect.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("RECTIFYMARKER"));
					Properties.PRect.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("RECTIFYMARKSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PDecanterTie.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("DECCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPLine>(out Properties.PDecanterTie.PLine, registryKey.GetValue("DECLINE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("DECMARKER"));
					Properties.PDecanterTie.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("DECMARKERSIZE"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPColour>(out Properties.PYTop.PColour, registryKey.GetValue("YTOPCOLOUR"));
					GetEnumFromObj<TPMarkerShape>(out Properties.PYTop.PMarker, registryKey.GetValue("YTOPMARKER"));
					Properties.PYTop.PMarkerSize = GetIntValueFromObj(registryKey.GetValue("YTOPMARKSIZE"));
					registryKey.Close();
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					registryKey.Close();
				}
			}
			registryKey = null;
		}

		public void Properties_Save()
		{
			//IL_080d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(REGKEY1, writable: true);
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					RegistryKey registryKey2 = registryKey.CreateSubKey(REGKEY2A + ASplitVersion.ASPENSPLIT_VERSION_STRING + REGKEY2B);
					if (registryKey2 != null)
					{
						registryKey.Close();
						registryKey2.SetValue("COORDINATES", CoDisplay, RegistryValueKind.String);
						registryKey2.SetValue("BOILINGPOINT", CalcBoilPoint, RegistryValueKind.String);
						registryKey2.SetValue("TRANSPARENTLABELS", TransparentLabels, RegistryValueKind.String);

		public IPlotobj FindLabelsParent(int iID)
		{
			//IL_0090: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IPlotobj result = null;
			try
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IPlotobj> item in cPObj)
				{
					if (item.Value.eObjType == ObjType.MarkUserPoint)
					{
						int.TryParse(item.Value.vUserData.ToString(), out var result2);
						if (result2 == iID)
						{
							result = item.Value;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MMMessageBox.Show(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, PlotFormsResources.PlotAreaFindParentLabelException, ex.Message), MMVersionHelper.MVGetI18nProductNameM(), (MMMessageBoxButton)0, (MMMessageBoxImage)0, false, 1, "apwn.chm", -1);
			}
			return result;
		}

	public enum ValidationKeyCodeEnum
	{
		KeyBlank = 0,
		KeyDelete = 31

	public class Validation
	{
		private const int KeyLowerE = 101;

		private const int KeyE = 69;

		private const int KeyDecimal = 46;

		private const int KeyComma = 44;

		private const int KeyNegative = 45;

		private int m_DecimalSeparator;

		private string m_DecimalSeparatorString;

		private long m_NumberOfExp;

		public bool InitValid { get; set; }

		public bool AllowNegative { get; set; }

		public bool AllowZero { get; set; }

		public bool ValidReal { get; set; }

		public string Value { get; set; }

		public long NumberOfExp
		{
			get
			{
				return m_NumberOfExp;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value < 1 || value > 3)
				{
					m_NumberOfExp = 2L;
				}
				else
				{
					m_NumberOfExp = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public Validation()
		{
			NumberOfExp = 2L;
			GetDecimalSeparator();
		}

		private void GetDecimalSeparator()
		{
			NumberFormatInfo numberFormat = Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat;
			m_DecimalSeparatorString = numberFormat.CurrencyDecimalSeparator;
			ASCIIEncoding aSCIIEncoding = new ASCIIEncoding();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(m_DecimalSeparatorString))
			{
				m_DecimalSeparator = aSCIIEncoding.GetBytes(m_DecimalSeparatorString)[0];
			}
			else
			{
				m_DecimalSeparator = 0;
			}
		}

		private void ValNewString(string NewString, ref int KeyAscii)
		{
			try
			{
				long num = NewString.IndexOf("-");
				long num2 = NewString.IndexOf("E");
				long num3 = NewString.IndexOf(".");
				if (NewString.Length == 0)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (MoreThanOne(69, NewString))
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (MoreThanOne(45, NewString))
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (MoreThanOne(m_DecimalSeparator, NewString))
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num2 != 0L && num != 0L && num != num2 + 1)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num2 != 0L && num == 0L && num2 < NewString.Length - NumberOfExp)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num != 0L && num2 == 0L && num < NewString.Length - NumberOfExp)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num2 != 0L && num != 0L && num < NewString.Length - NumberOfExp)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num3 != 0L && num2 != 0L && num3 > num2)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num3 != 0L && num != 0L && num3 > num)
				{
					KeyAscii = 0;
				}
				else if (num2 != 0L && num2 == 1)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (num2 != 0L && num2 == NewString.Length)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (num != 0L && num == NewString.Length)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (num2 == 0L && num != 0L)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (NewString == ".")
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (num != 0L && num2 == 0L && num2 < NewString.Length - NumberOfExp)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else if (double.Parse(NewString) == 0.0 && !AllowZero)
				{
					ValidReal = false;
				}
				else
				{
					ValidReal = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				ValidReal = false;
			}
		}

		private bool MoreThanOne(int KeyAscii, string MoreString)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				string value = new ASCIIEncoding().GetString(new byte[1] { (byte)KeyAscii });
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(MoreString))
				{
					result = false;
				}
				else
				{
					int num = MoreString.LastIndexOf(value);
					int num2 = MoreString.IndexOf(value);
					result = ((num != num2) ? true : false);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		[Obsolete]
		public void ValKeyPress(int KeyAscii, AesTextEdit Box)
		{
		}
	}
}
